    16	/**
    17	 * ADR-019 D1: THE ENGINE — one validator over declaration DATA, running
    18	 * the SAME declaration on the file-walk and direct-construction channels.
    19	 *
    20	 * The engine consumes a `SurfaceDecl`; it knows the VOCABULARY and knows
    21	 * nothing about any particular rule. A per-rule branch here (a literal
    22	 * `if (rule === X)`) is ADR-019 D9 tripwire #2 — a STOP and a scope
    23	 * review, never a work-around. Where two measured lanes disagree on a
    24	 * grain (path address, lane order, gating), the disagreement is DECLARED
    25	 * (`at:`, `laneOrder:`, `gating:`), which is the same instrument the
    26	 * audit's §5 F3 identified for path grain.
    27	 *
    28	 * Channel symmetry is structural, not argued (D1): attributes that read
    29	 * SOURCE text (`sourceForm`, the deep key-stringness scan) are declared
    30	 * once with `channel: "file"`; the engine runs them where a source exists
    31	 * and skips them where none does.
    32	 *
    33	 * This module VALIDATES and applies plain `default:` materialization. It
    34	 * does NOT derive values — derivation is the separately-named normalizer
    35	 * (D3, `normalizer.ts`).
    36	 */
    37	
    38	/** The engine's INTERNAL address for a declaration position
    39	 * (`$.steps.*.role`). It is NOT one of the declaration's three path
    40	 * languages (design review F11) — no declaration ever writes one; the walk
    41	 * builds them to key its reliability and completion bookkeeping. */
    42	type DeclPath = string;
    43	
    44	/** The channel the declaration is being run on. */
    45	export type EngineChannel =
    46	  | { readonly kind: "file"; readonly doc: Document; readonly source: string }
    47	  | { readonly kind: "direct" };
    48	
    49	export interface EngineOptions {
    50	  readonly channel: EngineChannel;
    51	  readonly catalog?: GateCatalog;
    52	}
    53	
    54	export interface EngineRun {
    55	  readonly findings: readonly ValidationFinding[];
    56	  /** The value with plain defaults materialized and file-channel maps
    57	   * realized as own-property records. Undefined when the root container
    58	   * lane failed (no operand exists). */
    59	  readonly normalized: unknown;
    60	  /** Binding path → the registration's EFFECTIVE config, collected as the
    61	   * `delegate` lanes resolve. The normalizer's `materializeEffectiveConfigs`
    62	   * hook consumes it. */
    63	  readonly effectiveConfigs: ReadonlyMap<string, unknown>;
    64	  /** Every binding whose resolved registration declares
    65	   * `requiresRuntimeContext` — the operand of the R3 residual cross-rule,
    66	   * which stays hand code (audit §4). */
    67	  readonly runtimeContextBindings: readonly string[];
    68	}
    69	
    70	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    71	// Value access: ONE accessor over both channels' container forms.
    72	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    73	
    74	type AnyMap = ReadonlyMap<unknown, unknown> | Record<string, unknown>;
    75	
    76	function isResolvedMap(value: unknown): value is ReadonlyMap<unknown, unknown> {
    77	  return value instanceof Map;
    78	}
    79	
    80	/** A container map on EITHER channel: the file channel's `mapAsMap` JS Map
    81	 * or the direct channel's plain object. Arrays are never maps. */
    82	function isContainer(value: unknown): value is AnyMap {
    83	  return typeof value === "object" && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value);
    84	}
    85	
    86	function entriesOf(value: AnyMap): readonly (readonly [unknown, unknown])[] {
    87	  if (isResolvedMap(value)) return [...value.entries()];
    88	  return Object.keys(value).map((key) => [key, ownGet(value, key)] as const);
    89	}
    90	
    91	function ownGet(record: Record<string, unknown>, key: string): unknown {
    92	  return Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(record, key) ? record[key] : undefined;
    93	}
    94	
    95	/** An OWN-property lookup in a declared registry keyed by AUTHORED text.
    96	 * A step id, an event type or a scalar value may legally be `constructor`
    97	 * or `toString`, and a bracket read would return the prototype's member —
    98	 * which is not a message, and calling it as one crashes the walk. The G8
    99	 * discipline this codebase already applies to value maps, applied to the
   100	 * declaration's own maps. */
   101	function ownMessage(
   102	  registry: Readonly<Record<string, MessageTemplate>> | undefined,
   103	  key: string,
   104	): MessageTemplate | undefined {
   105	  if (registry === undefined) return undefined;
   106	  return Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(registry, key) ? registry[key] : undefined;
   107	}
   108	
   109	function containerHas(value: AnyMap, key: string): boolean {
   110	  if (isResolvedMap(value)) return value.has(key);
   111	  return Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(value, key);
   112	}
   113	
   114	function containerGet(value: AnyMap, key: string): unknown {
   115	  if (isResolvedMap(value)) return value.get(key);
   116	  return ownGet(value, key);
   117	}
   118	
   119	/** A PLAIN string-keyed map — a plain object or null-prototype object,
   120	 * never an array, a JS Map, or a class instance (`map.plain`). */
   121	function isPlainMap(value: unknown): value is Record<string, unknown> {
   122	  if (typeof value !== "object" || value === null || Array.isArray(value)) return false;
   123	  const proto: unknown = Object.getPrototypeOf(value);
   124	  return proto === Object.prototype || proto === null;
   125	}
   126	
   127	/** Own-property WRITE: a step id or event type legally admits `__proto__`;
   128	 * a bracket assignment would set the prototype and drop the own key. */
   129	function defineOwn<T>(target: Record<string, T>, key: string, value: T): void {
   130	  Object.defineProperty(target, key, { configurable: true, enumerable: true, value, writable: true });
   131	}
   132	
   133	/** Realize a resolved value graph as own-property records where every map
   134	 * key is a string, preserving aliased identity through one memo. A map
   135	 * with any non-string key stays a JS Map (its identity class is what the
   136	 * key lanes report on). */
   137	function materialize(value: unknown, seen: WeakMap<object, unknown>): unknown {
   138	  if (typeof value !== "object" || value === null) return value;
   139	  const existing = seen.get(value);
   140	  if (existing !== undefined) return existing;
   141	  if (Array.isArray(value)) {
   142	    const result: unknown[] = [];
   143	    seen.set(value, result);
   144	    for (const item of value) result.push(materialize(item, seen));
   145	    return result;
   146	  }
   147	  if (isResolvedMap(value)) {
   148	    const entries = [...value.entries()];
   149	    if (entries.every(([key]) => typeof key === "string")) {
   150	      const record: Record<string, unknown> = {};
   151	      seen.set(value, record);
   152	      for (const [key, item] of entries) defineOwn(record, key as string, materialize(item, seen));
   153	      return record;
   154	    }
   155	    const map = new Map<unknown, unknown>();
   156	    seen.set(value, map);
   157	    for (const [key, item] of entries) map.set(materialize(key, seen), materialize(item, seen));
   158	    return map;
   159	  }
   160	  return value;
   161	}
   162	
   163	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   164	// Paths and message interpolation.
   165	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   166	
   167	/** `$` is the template root; `""` is a delegated config's own root. Both
   168	 * are addressed by their children's bare key (the measured path grammar). */
   169	function joinPath(parent: string, key: string): string {
   170	  return parent === "$" || parent === "" ? key : `${parent}.${key}`;
   171	}
   172	
   173	function indexPath(parent: string, index: number): string {
   174	  return `${parent}[${String(index)}]`;
   175	}
   176	
   177	/** The finding-message value rendering: objects are DESCRIBED, never
   178	 * serialized (a cyclic graph reaches the walk). */
   179	function describeValue(value: unknown): string {
   180	  if (typeof value === "object" && value !== null) {
   181	    return Array.isArray(value) ? "a list" : "a map";
   182	  }
   183	  return JSON.stringify(value) ?? String(value);
   184	}
   185	
   186	/** A key rendered for a message: a string key verbatim, anything else
   187	 * through the same description ladder values use (a non-string key is a
   188	 * real possibility on the file channel). */
   189	function describeKey(key: unknown): string {
   190	  return typeof key === "string" ? key : describeValue(key);
   191	}
   192	
   193	export interface Slots {
   194	  readonly path?: string | undefined;
   195	  readonly key?: unknown;
   196	  readonly value?: unknown;
   197	  readonly source?: string | undefined;
   198	  readonly grammar?: string | undefined;
   199	  readonly members?: string | undefined;
   200	  readonly keys?: string | undefined;
   201	  readonly label?: string | undefined;
   202	  readonly ownerKey?: string | undefined;
   203	}
   204	
   205	/** Interpolate a declared message template. The slot set is CLOSED and
   206	 * supplied by the engine from the node's own evaluation context — no slot
   207	 * carries rule-specific knowledge. */
   208	export function renderMessage(template: MessageTemplate, slots: Slots): string {
   209	  return render(template, slots);
   210	}
   211	
   212	function render(template: MessageTemplate, slots: Slots): string {
   213	  return template.replace(/\{(\w+)\}/gu, (whole, name: string): string => {
   214	    switch (name) {
   215	      case "path":
   216	        return slots.path ?? whole;
   217	      case "key":
   218	        return slots.key === undefined ? whole : describeKey(slots.key);
   219	      case "keyJson":
   220	        return JSON.stringify(slots.key) ?? whole;
   221	      case "value":
   222	        return "value" in slots ? describeValue(slots.value) : whole;
   223	      case "valueRaw":
   224	        return "value" in slots ? String(slots.value) : whole;
   225	      case "valueJson":
   226	        return "value" in slots ? (JSON.stringify(slots.value) ?? String(slots.value)) : whole;
   227	      case "source":
   228	        return slots.source ?? whole;
   229	      case "grammar":
   230	        return slots.grammar ?? whole;
   231	      case "members":
   232	        return slots.members ?? whole;
   233	      case "keys":
   234	        return slots.keys ?? whole;
   235	      case "label":
   236	        return slots.label ?? whole;
   237	      case "ownerKey":
   238	        return slots.ownerKey ?? whole;
   239	      default:
   240	        return whole;
   241	    }
   242	  });
   243	}
   244	
   245	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   246	// The run state.
   247	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   248	
   249	interface Frame {
   250	  /** The declaration path of the node being evaluated (`$.steps.*.role`). */
   251	  readonly decl: DeclPath;
   252	  /** The value path the findings address (`steps.review.role`). */
   253	  readonly path: string;
   254	  /** The SOURCE-document address, carried segment by segment as the walk
   255	   * descends. It is not derived from `path`: a rendered path is lossy —
   256	   * an authored id may itself contain `[0]` or a dot, and re-parsing one
   257	   * addresses a different node or none (B1 F3). */
   258	  readonly segments: readonly (string | number)[];
   259	  readonly value: unknown;
   260	  /** The enclosing container's value — the operand of a `..` selector. */
   261	  readonly parentValue: unknown;
   262	  /** The key of the nearest enclosing OPEN-map entry. */
   263	  readonly ownerKey?: string | undefined;
   264	  readonly label?: string | undefined;
   265	}
   266	
   267	/** What a node evaluation reports upward: whether the node produced a
   268	 * value its dependents may rely on (the implicit container precondition
   269	 * plus every DECLARED `gating`). */
   270	interface NodeResult {
   271	  readonly ok: boolean;
   272	  readonly value?: unknown;
   273	}
   274	
   275	/** A membership lane whose operand has NOT been evaluated yet. The engine
   276	 * defers it to the end of the walk rather than reading an operand whose
   277	 * reliability is still unknown — a GENERAL property of the `memberOf`
   278	 * construct, not a per-rule placement. */
   279	interface DeferredCheck {
   280	  readonly frame: Frame;
   281	  readonly rule: MembershipRule;
   282	  readonly candidate: unknown;
   283	  readonly slots: Slots;
   284	}
   285	
   286	class Run {
   287	  readonly findings: ValidationFinding[] = [];
   288	  readonly effectiveConfigs = new Map<string, unknown>();
   289	  readonly runtimeContextBindings: string[] = [];
   290	  /** Declaration path / tag → whether every instance produced a usable
   291	   * value. A rule naming an unreliable operand is suppressed. */
   292	  readonly declOk = new Map<string, boolean>();
   293	  /** Declaration paths whose evaluation has completed. An operand that is
   294	   * neither reliable nor unreliable but PENDING defers its rule. */
   295	  readonly completed = new Set<DeclPath>();
   296	  readonly deferred: DeferredCheck[] = [];
   297	  readonly memo = new WeakMap<object, unknown>();
   298	  root: unknown;
   299	
   300	  constructor(
   301	    readonly channel: EngineChannel,
   302	    readonly registries: Readonly<Record<string, GateCatalog | undefined>>,
   303	    readonly valueClasses: Readonly<Record<string, NodeDecl>>,
   304	  ) {}
   305	
   306	  /** The injected set a selector root or a delegation names. */
   307	  registry(name: string): GateCatalog | undefined {
   308	    return this.registries[name];
   309	  }
   310	
   311	  emit(path: string, message: string, code?: string): void {
   312	    this.findings.push(code === undefined ? { path, message } : { path, message, code });
   313	  }
   314	
   315	  mark(key: string, ok: boolean): void {
   316	    if (this.declOk.get(key) === false) return;
   317	    this.declOk.set(key, ok);
   318	  }
   319	
   320	  reliable(key: string): boolean {
   321	    return this.declOk.get(key) !== false;
   322	  }
   323	}
   324	
   325	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   326	// Selectors.
   327	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   328	
   329	type SelectorResult =
   330	  | { readonly status: "ok"; readonly values: readonly string[] }
   331	  | { readonly status: "unreliable" | "pending" };
   332	
   333	const UNRELIABLE = { status: "unreliable" } as const;
   334	const PENDING = { status: "pending" } as const;
   335	
   336	/** Walk a declaration path over the value graph, expanding `*` over open
   337	 * maps. `..` resolves one level up from the citing frame. A prefix that
   338	 * has not been evaluated yet answers PENDING, not "unreliable". */
   339	function resolvePath(
   340	  run: Run,
   341	  from: Frame,
   342	  path: DeclPath,
   343	): { readonly status: "ok"; readonly nodes: unknown[] } | { readonly status: "unreliable" | "pending" } {
   344	  const segments = path.split(".");
   345	  let nodes: unknown[];
   346	  let declPrefix: string;
   347	  const head = segments.shift();
   348	  if (head === "$") {
   349	    nodes = [run.root];
   350	    declPrefix = "$";
   351	  } else if (head === "^") {
   352	    // Parent-relative: the citing node's own container.
   353	    nodes = [from.parentValue];
   354	    declPrefix = from.decl.split(".").slice(0, -1).join(".");
   355	  } else {
   356	    return UNRELIABLE;
   357	  }
   358	  for (const segment of segments) {
   359	    declPrefix = `${declPrefix}.${segment}`;
   360	    if (!run.reliable(declPrefix)) return UNRELIABLE;
   361	    if (!run.completed.has(declPrefix)) return PENDING;
   362	    const next: unknown[] = [];
   363	    for (const node of nodes) {
   364	      if (!isContainer(node)) return UNRELIABLE;
   365	      if (segment === "*") {
   366	        for (const [key, child] of entriesOf(node)) {
   367	          if (typeof key === "string") next.push(child);
   368	        }
   369	      } else {
   370	        if (!containerHas(node, segment)) return UNRELIABLE;
   371	        next.push(containerGet(node, segment));
   372	      }
   373	    }
   374	    nodes = next;
   375	  }
   376	  return { status: "ok", nodes };
   377	}
   378	
   379	function evaluateSelector(run: Run, from: Frame, selector: Selector): SelectorResult {
   380	  if ("injected" in selector) {
   381	    // D8's ADMITTED widening: the selector root is an INJECTED set. A
   382	    // registry resolves rather than enumerates, so membership is asked of
   383	    // it directly (`isMember`); the set itself is never listed.
   384	    return run.registry(selector.injected) === undefined ? UNRELIABLE : { status: "ok", values: [] };
   385	  }
   386	  if ("union" in selector) {
   387	    const parts = selector.union.map((part) => evaluateSelector(run, from, part));
   388	    if (parts.some((part) => part.status === "pending")) return PENDING;
   389	    if (parts.some((part) => part.status === "unreliable")) return UNRELIABLE;
   390	    return {
   391	      status: "ok",
   392	      values: parts.flatMap((part) => (part.status === "ok" ? part.values : [])),
   393	    };
   394	  }
   395	  const target = "keysOf" in selector ? selector.keysOf : "valuesOf" in selector ? selector.valuesOf : selector.collect;
   396	  const resolved = resolvePath(run, from, target);
   397	  if (resolved.status !== "ok") return resolved;
   398	  const values: string[] = [];
   399	  for (const node of resolved.nodes) {
   400	    if ("keysOf" in selector) {
   401	      if (!isContainer(node)) return UNRELIABLE;
   402	      for (const [key] of entriesOf(node)) if (typeof key === "string") values.push(key);
   403	    } else if ("valuesOf" in selector) {
   404	      if (!Array.isArray(node)) return UNRELIABLE;
   405	      for (const item of node) if (typeof item === "string") values.push(item);
   406	    } else {
   407	      if (typeof node !== "string") return UNRELIABLE;
   408	      values.push(node);
   409	    }
   410	  }
   411	  return { status: "ok", values };
   412	}
   413	
   414	/** `memberOf` against an INJECTED root asks the registry; against a
   415	 * document root it asks the collected set. */
   416	function isMember(run: Run, selector: Selector, set: SelectorResult, candidate: string): boolean {
   417	  if ("injected" in selector) return run.registry(selector.injected)?.resolve(candidate) != null;
   418	  return set.status === "ok" && set.values.includes(candidate);
   419	}
   420	
   421	function checkMembership(
   422	  run: Run,
   423	  frame: Frame,
   424	  rule: MembershipRule,
   425	  candidate: unknown,
   426	  slots: Slots,
   427	  local?: ReadonlyMap<string, boolean>,
   428	): void {
   429	  if (rule.dependsOn?.some((tag) => local?.get(tag) === false || !run.reliable(tag)) === true) return;
   430	  const set = evaluateSelector(run, frame, rule.target);
   431	  if (set.status === "pending") {
   432	    run.deferred.push({ frame, rule, candidate, slots });
   433	    return;
   434	  }
   435	  if (set.status === "unreliable") return;
   436	  const at = rule.at === "container" ? parentPath(frame.path) : frame.path;
   437	  const hit = typeof candidate === "string" && isMember(run, rule.target, set, candidate);
   438	  const violated = rule.relation === "disjointFrom" ? hit : !hit;
   439	  if (violated) {
   440	    run.emit(at, render(rule.message, { ...slots, path: at, value: candidate }), rule.code);
   441	  }
   442	}
   443	
   444	function parentPath(path: string): string {
   445	  const bracket = path.lastIndexOf("[");
   446	  const dot = path.lastIndexOf(".");
   447	  if (bracket > dot) return path.slice(0, bracket);
   448	  if (dot < 0) return "$";
   449	  return path.slice(0, dot);
   450	}
   451	
   452	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   453	// The source-form ladder (vocabulary #6) — file channel only.
   454	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   455	
   456	const PLAIN_DECIMAL = /^[1-9][0-9]*$/;
   457	
   458	/**
   459	 * The `plainDecimalInteger` ladder over a YAML node: alias-free,
   460	 * scalar, anchor-free, tag-free, and a `^[1-9][0-9]*$` raw slice, then
   460	 * scalar, anchor-free, tag-free, and a `^[1-9][0-9]*$` raw slice, then
   461	 * the resolved safe-integer belt. Its six messages are FIXED by the
   462	 * ladder (they are identical at every measured user), so they live with
   463	 * the construct rather than being restated per node.
   464	 */
   465	const SOURCE_LADDER = {
   466	  alias: "{path} must not be an alias (an alias hides the source form, probe P21)",
   467	  nonScalar: "{path} must be a plain decimal integer scalar",
   468	  anchor: "{path} must not carry an anchor (the anchor token sits outside the range slice)",
   469	  tag: "{path} must not carry a tag (probe P22: a tag can flip the resolved type silently)",
   470	  form: "{path} must be written as a plain decimal integer >= 1; got source form {source}",
   471	  resolved: "{path} must resolve to a safe integer >= 1",
   472	} as const;
   473	
   474	export function sourceLadderFinding(node: unknown, source: string, path: string): string | undefined {
   475	  if (node === undefined || node === null) return undefined;
   476	  if (isAlias(node)) return render(SOURCE_LADDER.alias, { path });
   477	  if (!isScalar(node)) return render(SOURCE_LADDER.nonScalar, { path });
   478	  if (node.anchor !== undefined) return render(SOURCE_LADDER.anchor, { path });
   479	  if (node.tag !== undefined) return render(SOURCE_LADDER.tag, { path });
   480	  const range = node.range;
   481	  const slice = range ? source.slice(range[0], range[1]) : "";
   482	  if (!PLAIN_DECIMAL.test(slice)) {
   483	    return render(SOURCE_LADDER.form, { path, source: JSON.stringify(slice) });
   484	  }
   485	  const resolved: unknown = node.value;
   486	  if (typeof resolved !== "number" || !Number.isSafeInteger(resolved) || resolved < 1) {
   487	    return render(SOURCE_LADDER.resolved, { path });
   488	  }
   489	  return undefined;
   490	}
   491	
   492	
   493	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   494	// The acyclic-graph lane (the substrate's `resolve.graph`).
   495	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   496	
   497	/** DFS with a PATH-SCOPED ancestor set: shared subtrees (legal anchor
   498	 * reuse) are not cycles; only a back-edge is. */
   499	function findCycle(value: unknown, path: string, ancestors: Set<object>): string | undefined {
   500	  if (typeof value !== "object" || value === null) return undefined;
   501	  if (ancestors.has(value)) return path;
   502	  ancestors.add(value);
   503	  try {
   504	    if (Array.isArray(value)) {
   505	      for (let i = 0; i < value.length; i += 1) {
   506	        const hit = findCycle(value[i], joinPath(path, String(i)), ancestors);
   507	        if (hit !== undefined) return hit;
   508	      }
   509	      return undefined;
   510	    }
   511	    if (isResolvedMap(value)) {
   512	      for (const [key, child] of value.entries()) {
   513	        const segment = typeof key === "string" ? key : "<map-key>";
   514	        const keyHit = findCycle(key, joinPath(path, `${segment}<key>`), ancestors);
   515	        if (keyHit !== undefined) return keyHit;
   516	        const valueHit = findCycle(child, joinPath(path, segment), ancestors);
   517	        if (valueHit !== undefined) return valueHit;
   518	      }
   519	    }
   520	    return undefined;
   521	  } finally {
   522	    ancestors.delete(value);
   523	  }
   524	}
   525	
   526	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   527	// Node evaluation.
   528	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   529	
   530	const DEFAULT_MISSING = 'missing required key "{key}"';
   531	
   532	/** Sibling reliability inside ONE fixed-map evaluation. `dependsOn` reads
   533	 * it before the global map so a defect in binding #1 cannot suppress a
   534	 * lane in binding #2. */
   535	type LocalOk = Map<string, boolean>;
   536	
   537	function baseSlots(frame: Frame): Slots {
   538	  return { path: frame.path, value: frame.value, ownerKey: frame.ownerKey, label: frame.label };
   539	}
   540	
   541	function evaluateNode(run: Run, decl: NodeDecl, frame: Frame, local?: LocalOk): NodeResult {
   542	  const result = dispatch(run, decl, frame, local);
   543	  run.completed.add(frame.decl);
   544	  run.mark(frame.decl, result.ok);
   545	  run.mark(decl.tag, result.ok);
   546	  return result;
   547	}
   548	
   549	function dispatch(run: Run, decl: NodeDecl, frame: Frame, local?: LocalOk): NodeResult {
   550	  switch (decl.kind) {
   551	    case "map.fixed":
   552	      return evalMapFixed(run, decl, frame);
   553	    case "map.open":
   554	      return evalMapOpen(run, decl, frame);
   555	    case "map.plain":
   556	      return evalMapPlain(run, decl, frame);
   557	    case "list":
   558	      return evalList(run, decl, frame, local);
   559	    case "string":
   560	      return evalString(run, decl, frame, local);
   561	    case "integer":
   562	      return evalInteger(run, decl, frame);
   563	    case "enum":
   564	      return evalEnum(run, decl, frame);
   565	    case "union":
   566	      return evalUnion(run, decl, frame);
   567	    case "raw":
   568	      return evalRaw(run, decl, frame);
   569	    case "valueClass":
   570	      return evalValueClassRef(run, decl, frame, local);
   571	    case "delegate":
   572	      return evalDelegate(run, decl, frame, local);
   573	  }
   574	}
   575	
   576	function evalValueClassRef(
   577	  run: Run,
   578	  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "valueClass" }>,
   579	  frame: Frame,
   580	  local?: LocalOk,
   581	): NodeResult {
   582	  const target = run.valueClasses[decl.valueClass];
   583	  if (target === undefined) return { ok: true, value: frame.value };
   584	  const merged: Frame = { ...frame, label: decl.label ?? frame.label };
   585	  const result = dispatch(run, target, merged, local);
   586	  // The REFERENCING site's gating decides, not the shared class's.
   587	  if (!result.ok && decl.gating === true) run.mark(frame.decl, false);
   588	  return result;
   589	}
   590	
   591	function evalMapFixed(
   592	  run: Run,
   593	  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "map.fixed" }>,
   594	  frame: Frame,
   595	): NodeResult {
   596	  const value = frame.value;
   597	  const slots = baseSlots(frame);
   598	  if (!isContainer(value)) {
   599	    run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.containerMessage, slots));
   600	    return { ok: false };
   601	  }
   602	  const container = value;
   603	  const fieldNames = Object.keys(decl.fields);
   604	  // A field declared for ONE channel is legal only there — the same
   605	  // channel scoping D1 applies to source-bearing attributes, applied to
   606	  // keyset membership.
   607	  const inChannel = (name: string): boolean => {
   608	    const channel = decl.fields[name]?.channel;
   609	    return channel === undefined || channel === "both" || channel === run.channel.kind;
   610	  };
   611	  // A channel-scoped field is scoped WHOLE: out of channel it is neither a
   612	  // legal key, nor a missing one, nor evaluated (design review F7).
   613	  const scoped = fieldNames.filter(inChannel);
   614	  const legal = new Set(scoped);
   615	  const normalized: Record<string, unknown> = {};
   616	  const local: LocalOk = new Map();
   617	
   618	  const emitUnknownKeys = (): void => {
   619	    for (const [key] of entriesOf(container)) {
   620	      if (typeof key !== "string") {
   621	        run.emit(
   622	          frame.path,
   623	          render(decl.unknownMessage, { ...slots, key, value: key, keys: scoped.join(", ") }),
   624	        );
   625	        continue;
   626	      }
   627	      if (legal.has(key)) continue;
   628	      const at = joinPath(frame.path, key);
   629	      const removed = ownMessage(decl.removedKeys, key);
   630	      const keySlots: Slots = { ...slots, path: at, key, value: key, keys: scoped.join(", ") };
   631	      run.emit(at, render(removed ?? decl.unknownMessage, keySlots));
   632	    }
   633	  };
   634	
   635	  const emitMissing = (name: string): void => {
   636	    const field = decl.fields[name];
   637	    const presence = field?.presence;
   638	    if (field === undefined || presence?.required !== true) return;
   639	    if (containerHas(container, name)) return;
   640	    if (presence.foldedIntoTypeLane === true) {
   641	      // Absence is this node's OWN type lane (a binding's `uses`).
   642	      evaluateNode(run, field, childFrame(frame, name, undefined, container), local);
   643	      local.set(field.tag, false);
   644	      return;
   645	    }
   646	    const at = presence.at === "self" ? joinPath(frame.path, name) : frame.path;
   647	    run.emit(
   648	      at,
   649	      render(presence.message ?? decl.missingMessage ?? DEFAULT_MISSING, { ...slots, path: at, key: name }),
   650	      presence.code,
   651	    );
   652	    local.set(field.tag, false);
   653	  };
   654	
   655	  const evalOneField = (name: string): void => {
   656	    const field = decl.fields[name];
   657	    if (field === undefined) return;
   658	    if (!containerHas(container, name)) {
   659	      // An ABSENT field is where a declared `default:` materializes — the
   660	      // schema-side half of ADR-019 D3.
   661	      if (field.default !== undefined) defineOwn(normalized, name, field.default);
   662	      return;
   663	    }
   664	    const child = childFrame(frame, name, containerGet(container, name), container);
   665	    const result = evaluateNode(run, field, child, local);
   666	    local.set(field.tag, result.ok);
   667	    if (result.ok && result.value !== undefined) defineOwn(normalized, name, result.value);
   668	  };
   669	
   670	  const order = decl.laneOrder ?? "unknownThenPerKey";
   671	  if (order === "missingThenUnknown") {
   672	    for (const name of scoped) emitMissing(name);
   673	    emitUnknownKeys();
   674	    for (const name of scoped) evalOneField(name);
   675	  } else if (order === "unknownThenMissingThenValues") {
   676	    emitUnknownKeys();
   677	    for (const name of scoped) emitMissing(name);
   678	    for (const name of scoped) evalOneField(name);
   679	  } else {
   680	    emitUnknownKeys();
   681	    for (const name of scoped) {
   682	      if (!containerHas(container, name)) {
   683	        emitMissing(name);
   684	        evalOneField(name);
   685	        continue;
   686	      }
   687	      evalOneField(name);
   688	    }
   689	  }
   690	  return { ok: true, value: normalized };
   691	}
   692	
   693	function childFrame(parent: Frame, key: string, value: unknown, parentValue: unknown): Frame {
   694	  return {
   695	    decl: `${parent.decl}.${key}`,
   696	    path: joinPath(parent.path, key),
   697	    segments: [...parent.segments, key],
   698	    value,
   699	    parentValue,
   700	    ownerKey: parent.ownerKey,
   701	  };
   702	}
   703	
   704	function evalMapOpen(
   705	  run: Run,
   706	  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "map.open" }>,
   707	  frame: Frame,
   708	): NodeResult {
   709	  const value = frame.value;
   710	  const slots = baseSlots(frame);
   711	  if (!isContainer(value)) {
   712	    run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.containerMessage, slots));
   713	    return { ok: false };
   714	  }
   715	  const container = value;
   716	  const entries = entriesOf(container);
   717	  let ok = true;
   718	  if (decl.nonempty !== undefined && entries.length === 0) {
   719	    run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.nonempty.message, slots));
   720	    if (decl.nonempty.gating === true) ok = false;
   721	  }
   722	  if (decl.deepKeyStringness?.channel === run.channel.kind) {
   723	    scanKeyStringness(run, container, frame.path, decl.deepKeyStringness.message, new WeakSet());
   724	  }
   725	  const normalized: Record<string, unknown> = {};
   726	  for (const [key, child] of entries) {
   727	    const keyIsString = typeof key === "string";
   728	    const entryPath = keyIsString ? joinPath(frame.path, key) : frame.path;
   729	    if (decl.keyClass !== undefined) {
   730	      const keyFrame: Frame = {
   731	        decl: `${frame.decl}<key>`,
   732	        path: decl.keyLaneAt === "container" ? frame.path : entryPath,
   733	        segments: frame.segments,
   734	        value: key,
   735	        parentValue: container,
   736	        ownerKey: frame.ownerKey,
   737	      };
   738	      const keyResult = evaluateNode(run, decl.keyClass, keyFrame);
   739	      if (!keyResult.ok && decl.keyClass.gating === true) ok = false;
   740	    }
   741	    if (decl.keysSubsetOf !== undefined) {
   742	      const before = run.findings.length;
   743	      // The rule is the OPEN MAP's, so a `^`-relative selector resolves
   744	      // from the map's own container — never from the map itself.
   745	      const subsetFrame: Frame = {
   746	        decl: frame.decl,
   747	        path: entryPath,
   748	        segments: frame.segments,
   749	        value: key,
   750	        parentValue: frame.parentValue,
   751	        ownerKey: frame.ownerKey,
   752	      };
   753	      checkMembership(run, subsetFrame, decl.keysSubsetOf, key, {
   754	        ...slots,
   755	        path: entryPath,
   756	        key,
   757	        ownerKey: frame.ownerKey,
   758	      });
   759	      // A key outside the subset is DEAD config: nothing below it has an
   760	      // operand to be validated against (the measured lane's CONTINUE).
   760	      // operand to be validated against (the measured lane's CONTINUE).
   761	      if (run.findings.length > before) continue;
   762	    }
   763	    const entryFrame: Frame = {
   764	      decl: `${frame.decl}.*`,
   765	      path: entryPath,
   766	      segments: keyIsString ? [...frame.segments, key] : frame.segments,
   767	      value: child,
   768	      parentValue: container,
   769	      ownerKey: keyIsString ? key : frame.ownerKey,
   770	    };
   771	    const entryResult = evaluateNode(run, decl.entry, entryFrame);
   772	    if (keyIsString && entryResult.ok && entryResult.value !== undefined) {
   773	      defineOwn(normalized, key, entryResult.value);
   774	    }
   775	  }
   776	  run.completed.add(`${frame.decl}.*`);
   777	  return { ok, value: normalized };
   778	}
   779	
   780	/** The gates subtree's file-channel key-STRINGNESS scan: a non-string key
   781	 * cannot become an own property and would blind every own-key scan, so it
   782	 * reports at the NEAREST addressable path (the containing map's). */
   783	function scanKeyStringness(
   784	  run: Run,
   785	  value: unknown,
   786	  path: string,
   787	  message: MessageTemplate,
   788	  seen: WeakSet<object>,
   789	): void {
   790	  if (typeof value !== "object" || value === null) return;
   791	  if (seen.has(value)) return;
   792	  seen.add(value);
   793	  if (Array.isArray(value)) {
   794	    value.forEach((item, index) => scanKeyStringness(run, item, indexPath(path, index), message, seen));
   795	    return;
   796	  }
   797	  if (!isResolvedMap(value)) return;
   798	  for (const [key, child] of value.entries()) {
   799	    if (typeof key !== "string") {
   800	      run.emit(path, render(message, { path, key, value: key }));
   801	      continue;
   802	    }
   803	    scanKeyStringness(run, child, joinPath(path, key), message, seen);
   804	  }
   805	}
   806	
   807	function evalList(
   808	  run: Run,
   809	  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "list" }>,
   810	  frame: Frame,
   811	  local?: LocalOk,
   812	): NodeResult {
   813	  const value = frame.value;
   814	  const slots = baseSlots(frame);
   815	  if (!Array.isArray(value)) {
   816	    run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.containerMessage, slots));
   817	    return { ok: false };
   818	  }
   819	  let ok = true;
   820	  if (decl.nonempty !== undefined && value.length === 0) {
   821	    run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.nonempty.message, slots));
   822	    if (decl.nonempty.gating === true) ok = false;
   823	  }
   824	  const normalized: unknown[] = [];
   825	  const seen = new Set<unknown>();
   826	  const clean: { readonly member: unknown; readonly path: string }[] = [];
   827	  value.forEach((member, index) => {
   828	    const memberPath = decl.memberLaneAt === "container" ? frame.path : indexPath(frame.path, index);
   829	    const memberFrame: Frame = {
   830	      decl: `${frame.decl}[]`,
   831	      path: memberPath,
   832	      segments: [...frame.segments, index],
   833	      value: member,
   834	      parentValue: value,
   835	      ownerKey: frame.ownerKey,
   836	    };
   837	    const before = run.findings.length;
   838	    const result = evaluateNode(run, decl.member, memberFrame, local);
   839	    if (run.findings.length > before) return;
   840	    if (result.value !== undefined) normalized.push(result.value);
   841	    if (decl.memberOf !== undefined) {
   842	      checkMembership(run, memberFrame, decl.memberOf, member, { ...slots, path: memberPath, value: member });
   843	    }
   844	    if (decl.unique?.grain === "perOccurrence") {
   845	      const at = decl.unique.at === "container" ? frame.path : memberPath;
   846	      if (seen.has(member)) {
   847	        run.emit(at, render(decl.unique.message, { ...slots, path: at, value: member }));
   848	      }
   849	      seen.add(member);
   850	    }
   851	    clean.push({ member, path: memberPath });
   852	  });
   853	  if (decl.disjointFrom !== undefined) {
   854	    for (const entry of clean) {
   855	      const memberFrame: Frame = {
   856	        decl: `${frame.decl}[]`,
   857	        path: entry.path,
   858	        segments: frame.segments,
   859	        value: entry.member,
   860	        parentValue: value,
   861	        ownerKey: frame.ownerKey,
   862	      };
   863	      checkMembership(run, memberFrame, decl.disjointFrom, entry.member, {
   864	        ...slots,
   865	        path: entry.path,
   866	        value: entry.member,
   867	      });
   868	    }
   869	  }
   870	  run.completed.add(`${frame.decl}[]`);
   871	  return { ok, value: normalized };
   872	}
   873	
   874	function evalString(
   875	  run: Run,
   876	  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "string" }>,
   877	  frame: Frame,
   878	  local?: LocalOk,
   879	): NodeResult {
   880	  const value = frame.value;
   881	  const slots: Slots = { ...baseSlots(frame), grammar: decl.grammar?.re };
   882	  if (typeof value !== "string") {
   883	    // A node that declares NO type lane leaves the non-string case to its
   884	    // membership lane, which emits ONE finding for both faults (the
   885	    // measured form of `start` and of a transition target).
   886	    if (decl.typeMessage !== undefined) {
   887	      run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.typeMessage, slots), decl.presence?.code);
   888	      return { ok: false };
   889	    }
   890	  } else {
   891	    if (decl.nonempty !== undefined && value.length === 0) {
   892	      run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.nonempty.message, slots), decl.presence?.code);
   893	      return { ok: false };
   894	    }
   895	    if (decl.grammar !== undefined && !new RegExp(decl.grammar.re, "u").test(value)) {
   896	      run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.grammar.message, slots));
   897	      return { ok: false };
   898	    }
   899	  }
   900	  if (decl.memberOf !== undefined) {
   901	    const before = run.findings.length;
   902	    checkMembership(run, frame, decl.memberOf, value, slots, local);
   903	    if (run.findings.length > before) return { ok: false, value };
   904	  }
   905	  return typeof value === "string" ? { ok: true, value } : { ok: false };
   906	}
   907	
   908	function evalInteger(
   909	  run: Run,
   910	  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "integer" }>,
   911	  frame: Frame,
   912	): NodeResult {
   913	  const value = frame.value;
   914	  if (decl.sourceForm === "plainDecimalInteger" && run.channel.kind === "file") {
   915	    const node: unknown = run.channel.doc.getIn(frame.segments, true);
   916	    const message = sourceLadderFinding(node, run.channel.source, frame.path);
   917	    if (message !== undefined) {
   918	      run.emit(frame.path, message);
   919	      return { ok: false };
   920	    }
   921	    return { ok: true, value };
   922	  }
   923	  const belt = decl.resolvedForm;
   924	  if (belt?.safeInteger === true) {
   925	    if (typeof value !== "number" || !Number.isSafeInteger(value) || value < belt.min) {
   926	      run.emit(frame.path, render(belt.message, baseSlots(frame)), decl.presence?.code);
   927	      return { ok: false };
   928	    }
   929	  }
   930	  return { ok: true, value };
   931	}
   932	
   933	function evalEnum(
   934	  run: Run,
   935	  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "enum" }>,
   936	  frame: Frame,
   937	): NodeResult {
   938	  const value = frame.value;
   939	  const legal = decl.members.filter(
   940	    (one) => one.channel === undefined || one.channel === "both" || one.channel === run.channel.kind,
   941	  );
   942	  const slots: Slots = {
   943	    ...baseSlots(frame),
   944	    members: legal.map((one) => String(one.value)).join(", "),
   945	  };
   946	  const member = legal.find((one) => one.value === value);
   947	  if (member !== undefined) return { ok: true, value: member.store ?? member.value };
   948	  run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.message, slots), decl.code);
   949	  return { ok: false };
   950	}
   951	
   952	function evalUnion(
   953	  run: Run,
   954	  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "union" }>,
   955	  frame: Frame,
   956	): NodeResult {
   957	  const value = frame.value;
   958	  const slots = baseSlots(frame);
   959	  if (typeof value === "string") {
   960	    const removed = ownMessage(decl.removedValues, value);
   961	    if (removed !== undefined) {
   962	      run.emit(frame.path, render(removed, slots));
   963	      return { ok: false };
   964	    }
   965	    if (decl.literals?.includes(value) === true) return { ok: true, value };
   966	  }
   967	  if (isContainer(value) && decl.mapCase !== undefined) {
   968	    return evaluateNode(run, decl.mapCase, frame);
   969	  }
   970	  run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.message, slots));
   971	  return { ok: false };
   972	}
   973	
   974	function evalRaw(run: Run, decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "raw" }>, frame: Frame): NodeResult {
   975	  if (decl.containerMessage !== undefined && !isContainer(frame.value)) {
   976	    run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.containerMessage, baseSlots(frame)));
   977	    return { ok: false };
   978	  }
   979	  return { ok: true, value: materialize(frame.value, run.memo) };
   980	}
   981	
   982	function evalMapPlain(
   983	  run: Run,
   984	  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "map.plain" }>,
   985	  frame: Frame,
   986	): NodeResult {
   987	  const value = frame.value;
   988	  const slots = baseSlots(frame);
   989	  const realized = materialize(value, run.memo);
   990	  if (!isPlainMap(realized)) {
   991	    run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.containerMessage, { ...slots, value: realized }));
   992	    return { ok: false };
   993	  }
   994	  if (!isCanonicalizable(realized)) {
   995	    run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.canonicalJsonSafe.message, slots));
   996	    return { ok: false };
   997	  }
   998	  return { ok: true, value: realized };
   999	}
  1000	
  1001	function evalDelegate(
  1002	  run: Run,
  1003	  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "delegate" }>,
  1004	  frame: Frame,
  1005	  local?: LocalOk,
  1006	): NodeResult {
  1007	  if (decl.dependsOn?.some((tag) => local?.get(tag) === false) === true) return { ok: true };
  1008	  const owner = frame.parentValue;
  1009	  const by = isContainer(owner) ? containerGet(owner, decl.by) : undefined;
  1010	  if (typeof by !== "string") return { ok: true };
  1011	  const registration = run.registry(decl.registry)?.resolve(by) ?? null;
  1012	  if (registration === null) return { ok: true };
  1013	  if (registration.requiresRuntimeContext) run.runtimeContextBindings.push(parentPath(frame.path));
  1014	  const raw = frame.value === undefined ? undefined : materialize(frame.value, run.memo);
  1015	  const result = registration.validateAndNormalizeConfig(raw);
  1016	  if (result.ok) {
  1017	    run.effectiveConfigs.set(parentPath(frame.path), result.effective);
  1018	    return { ok: true, value: result.effective };
  1019	  }
  1020	  if (result.findings.length === 0) {
  1021	    run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.beltMessage, { ...baseSlots(frame), key: by, value: by }));
  1022	    return { ok: false };
  1023	  }
  1024	  for (const finding of result.findings) {
  1025	    const at = finding.path === "" ? frame.path : `${frame.path}.${finding.path}`;
  1026	    run.emit(at, finding.message, finding.code);
  1027	  }
  1028	  return { ok: false };
  1029	}
  1030	
  1031	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  1032	// Cross rules and the deferred queue.
  1033	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  1034	
  1035	function unique(values: readonly string[]): string[] {
  1036	  return [...new Set(values)];
  1037	}
  1038	
  1039	/** vocabulary #12's `equals` — two-direction set equality, each direction
  1040	 * carrying its own path grain and wording (ch8-C16's measured form). */
  1041	function evaluateEquals(run: Run, frame: Frame, rule: EqualsRuleDecl): void {
  1042	  if (rule.dependsOn?.some((tag) => !run.reliable(tag)) === true) return;
  1043	  const left = evaluateSelector(run, frame, rule.left);
  1044	  const right = evaluateSelector(run, frame, rule.right);
  1045	  if (left.status !== "ok" || right.status !== "ok") return;
  1046	  const declared = unique(left.values);
  1047	  const used = unique(right.values);
  1048	  for (const value of used) {
  1049	    if (declared.includes(value)) continue;
  1050	    const at = render(rule.missingFromLeft.at, { value });
  1051	    run.emit(at, render(rule.missingFromLeft.message, { path: at, value }));
  1052	  }
  1053	  for (const value of declared) {
  1054	    if (used.includes(value)) continue;
  1055	    const at = render(rule.missingFromRight.at, { value });
  1056	    run.emit(at, render(rule.missingFromRight.message, { path: at, value }));
  1057	  }
  1058	}
  1059	
  1060	function runDeferred(run: Run): void {
  1061	  // A membership lane deferred for a PENDING operand runs once, in the
  1062	  // order the walk queued it. The queue is drained, never re-fed: every
  1063	  // operand has completed by now, so a second pass could only loop.
  1064	  const queued = run.deferred.splice(0, run.deferred.length);
  1065	  for (const check of queued) {
  1066	    checkMembership(run, check.frame, check.rule, check.candidate, check.slots);
  1067	  }
  1068	  run.deferred.length = 0;
  1069	}
  1070	
  1071	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  1072	// Entry points.
  1073	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  1074	
  1075	/**
  1076	 * Run a surface declaration over a value graph. ONE computation serves
  1077	 * both channels (D1): the caller supplies `channel`, and the source-bearing
  1078	 * attributes are inert where no source exists.
  1079	 */
  1080	export function runSurface(surface: SurfaceDecl, value: unknown, opts: EngineOptions): EngineRun {
  1081	  const run = new Run(opts.channel, { gateCatalog: opts.catalog }, surface.valueClasses);
  1082	  run.root = value;
  1083	  const rootFrame: Frame = { decl: "$", path: "$", segments: [], value, parentValue: undefined };
  1084	  const rootDecl = surface.root;
  1085	
  1086	  // The root container precondition: with no map operand nothing below has
  1087	  // an address, so the ONE finding is the whole result. It applies where
  1088	  // the ROOT is declared as a map — a surface whose root is any other
  1089	  // kind evaluates through its own node like every other.
  1090	  const rootIsMap =
  1091	    rootDecl.kind === "map.fixed" || rootDecl.kind === "map.open" || rootDecl.kind === "map.plain";
  1092	  if (rootIsMap && !isContainer(value)) {
  1093	    run.emit("$", render(rootDecl.containerMessage, { path: "$", value }));
  1094	    return {
  1095	      findings: run.findings,
  1096	      normalized: undefined,
  1097	      effectiveConfigs: run.effectiveConfigs,
  1098	      runtimeContextBindings: run.runtimeContextBindings,
  1099	    };
  1100	  }
  1101	
  1102	  // The substrate's `resolve.graph: acyclic` lane. It ACCUMULATES with the
  1103	  // structural lanes (a cycle is not a container, so it suppresses
  1104	  // nothing), and it has an operand only where a resolved graph exists.
  1105	  if (run.channel.kind === "file") {
  1106	    const cycle = findCycle(value, "$", new Set());
  1107	    if (cycle !== undefined) {
  1108	      run.emit(cycle, render(surface.substrate.resolve.graph.message, { path: cycle }));
  1109	    }
  1110	  }
  1111	
  1112	  const result = evaluateNode(run, rootDecl, rootFrame);
  1113	  runDeferred(run);
  1114	  for (const rule of surface.crossRules) {
  1115	    if (rule.relation === "equals") evaluateEquals(run, rootFrame, rule);
  1116	  }
  1117	  return {
  1118	    findings: run.findings,
  1119	    normalized: result.value,
  1120	    effectiveConfigs: run.effectiveConfigs,
  1121	    runtimeContextBindings: run.runtimeContextBindings,
  1122	  };
  1123	}
     1	import type { NormalizerHookDecl, SurfaceDecl } from "./vocabulary.js";
     2	
     3	/**
     4	 * ADR-019 D3: THE NORMALIZER — the engine's SECOND capability, named so
     5	 * that "schema" is never read as covering it.
     6	 *
     7	 * A declaration says what is LEGAL; it does not compute a value. Admission
     8	 * both validates and PRODUCES the admitted form (the audit's residual R4:
     9	 * `advancesRound` expanded per transition, the effective gate config
    10	 * materialized). Those are TRANSFORMS, and they live here.
    11	 *
    12	 * The boundary is exact and load-bearing:
    13	 * - plain `default:` materialization IS declarable and stays SCHEMA-side
    14	 *   (the engine applies it while walking — `activation`, `runtimeContext`,
    15	 *   `output.mode`, the disposition and reason defaults);
    16	 * - DERIVATION — a value computed from other values — is the normalizer's.
    17	 *
    18	 * Each hook is a NAMED capability parameterized by declared operand paths,
    19	 * so adding one is D7 format growth (a ratified amendment), never an edit
    20	 * that slips in under "normalization".
    21	 */
    22	
    23	/** Own-property WRITE: a step id or event type legally admits `__proto__`. */
    24	function defineOwn<T>(target: Record<string, T>, key: string, value: T): void {
    25	  Object.defineProperty(target, key, { configurable: true, enumerable: true, value, writable: true });
    26	}
    27	
    28	function isRecord(value: unknown): value is Record<string, unknown> {
    29	  return typeof value === "object" && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value);
    30	}
    31	
    32	function ownGet(record: Record<string, unknown>, key: string): unknown {
    33	  return Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(record, key) ? record[key] : undefined;
    34	}
    35	
    36	/** Resolve a declared operand path (`$.steps`, `$.round.advanceOnArrivalAt`)
    37	 * over the normalized value. */
    38	function at(root: unknown, path: string): unknown {
    39	  let cursor: unknown = root;
    40	  for (const segment of path.split(".")) {
    41	    if (segment === "$") continue;
    42	    if (!isRecord(cursor)) return undefined;
    43	    cursor = ownGet(cursor, segment);
    44	  }
    45	  return cursor;
    46	}
    47	
    48	/**
    49	 * R4's first member (ch11-C39): expand each entry's edge map into a
    50	 * COMPLETE per-edge boolean map against the declared advancing set. An
    51	 * absent declaration yields all-false (ch11-C38's none-default); the
    52	 * PRODUCER MONOPOLY holds — the map is recomputed and overwrites whatever
    53	 * the input carried.
    54	 */
    55	function expandFlagMaps(
    56	  root: Record<string, unknown>,
    57	  hook: Extract<NormalizerHookDecl, { hook: "expandAdvancesRound" }>,
    58	): void {
    59	  const entries = at(root, hook.over);
    60	  if (!isRecord(entries)) return;
    61	  const declared = at(root, hook.advanceSet);
    62	  const advancing = new Set<string>(Array.isArray(declared) ? (declared as string[]) : []);
    63	  for (const key of Object.keys(entries)) {
    64	    const entry = ownGet(entries, key);
    65	    if (!isRecord(entry)) continue;
    66	    const edges = ownGet(entry, hook.edges);
    67	    const flags: Record<string, boolean> = {};
    68	    if (isRecord(edges)) {
    69	      for (const edge of Object.keys(edges)) {
    70	        defineOwn(flags, edge, advancing.has(ownGet(edges, edge) as string));
    71	      }
    72	    }
    73	    defineOwn(entry, hook.into, flags);
    74	  }
    75	}
    76	
    77	/**
    78	 * R4's second member (ch11-C20/C5): write each registration's EFFECTIVE
    79	 * config into the binding's single config surface. The binding is rebuilt
    80	 * from its CARRIED fields plus the effective config — the one downstream
    81	 * config form.
    82	 */
    83	function materializeConfigs(
    84	  root: Record<string, unknown>,
    85	  hook: Extract<NormalizerHookDecl, { hook: "materializeEffectiveConfigs" }>,
    86	  effectiveConfigs: ReadonlyMap<string, unknown>,
    87	): void {
    88	  // `over` addresses the pipeline map through two open-map levels:
    89	  // `$.steps.*.gates` — the entry key and the event key are the wildcards.
    90	  const [ownerPath, ...rest] = hook.over.split(".*.");
    91	  const owners = at(root, ownerPath ?? "$");
    92	  if (!isRecord(owners)) return;
    93	  const inner = rest.join(".*.");
    94	  for (const ownerKey of Object.keys(owners)) {
    95	    const owner = ownGet(owners, ownerKey);
    96	    if (!isRecord(owner)) continue;
    97	    const pipelines = ownGet(owner, inner);
    98	    if (!isRecord(pipelines)) continue;
    99	    const rebuiltPipelines: Record<string, unknown> = {};
   100	    for (const eventKey of Object.keys(pipelines)) {
   101	      const pipeline = ownGet(pipelines, eventKey);
   102	      if (!Array.isArray(pipeline)) continue;
   103	      const base = `${ownerPath === "$" ? "" : `${(ownerPath ?? "").replace("$.", "")}.`}${ownerKey}.${inner}.${eventKey}`;
   104	      defineOwn(
   105	        rebuiltPipelines,
   106	        eventKey,
   107	        pipeline.map((binding: unknown, index: number) => {
   108	          const carried: Record<string, unknown> = {};
   109	          if (isRecord(binding)) {
   110	            for (const field of hook.carry) defineOwn(carried, field, ownGet(binding, field));
   111	          }
   112	          defineOwn(carried, hook.into, effectiveConfigs.get(`${base}[${String(index)}]`));
   113	          return carried;
   114	        }),
   115	      );
   116	    }
   117	    defineOwn(owner, inner, rebuiltPipelines);
   118	  }
   119	}
   120	
   121	/**
   122	 * Run every declared normalizer hook over a VALIDATED value. The caller
   123	 * guarantees the value carries no findings — a declaration that refused
   124	 * the input never reaches derivation.
   125	 */
   126	export function normalize(
   127	  surface: SurfaceDecl,
   128	  value: unknown,
   129	  effectiveConfigs: ReadonlyMap<string, unknown>,
   130	): unknown {
   131	  if (!isRecord(value)) return value;
   132	  for (const hook of surface.normalizers) {
   133	    if (hook.hook === "expandAdvancesRound") {
   134	      expandFlagMaps(value, hook);
   135	      continue;
   136	    }
   137	    materializeConfigs(value, hook, effectiveConfigs);
   138	  }
   139	  return value;
   140	}

   421	function checkMembership(
   422	  run: Run,
   423	  frame: Frame,
   424	  rule: MembershipRule,
   425	  candidate: unknown,
   426	  slots: Slots,
   427	  local?: ReadonlyMap<string, boolean>,
   428	): void {
   429	  if (rule.dependsOn?.some((tag) => local?.get(tag) === false || !run.reliable(tag)) === true) return;
   430	  const set = evaluateSelector(run, frame, rule.target);
   431	  if (set.status === "pending") {
   432	    run.deferred.push({ frame, rule, candidate, slots });
   433	    return;
   434	  }
   435	  if (set.status === "unreliable") return;
   436	  const at = rule.at === "container" ? parentPath(frame.path) : frame.path;
   437	  const hit = typeof candidate === "string" && isMember(run, rule.target, set, candidate);
   438	  const violated = rule.relation === "disjointFrom" ? hit : !hit;
   439	  if (violated) {
   440	    run.emit(at, render(rule.message, { ...slots, path: at, value: candidate }), rule.code);
   441	  }
   442	}
   443	
   444	function parentPath(path: string): string {
   445	  const bracket = path.lastIndexOf("[");
   446	  const dot = path.lastIndexOf(".");
   447	  if (bracket > dot) return path.slice(0, bracket);
   448	  if (dot < 0) return "$";
   449	  return path.slice(0, dot);
   450	}
   451	
   452	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   453	// The source-form ladder (vocabulary #6) — file channel only.
   454	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   455	
   456	const PLAIN_DECIMAL = /^[1-9][0-9]*$/;
   457	
   458	/**
   459	 * The `plainDecimalInteger` ladder over a YAML node: alias-free,
   460	 * scalar, anchor-free, tag-free, and a `^[1-9][0-9]*$` raw slice, then
   461	 * the resolved safe-integer belt. Its six messages are FIXED by the
   462	 * ladder (they are identical at every measured user), so they live with
   463	 * the construct rather than being restated per node.
   464	 */
   465	const SOURCE_LADDER = {
   466	  alias: "{path} must not be an alias (an alias hides the source form, probe P21)",
   467	  nonScalar: "{path} must be a plain decimal integer scalar",
   468	  anchor: "{path} must not carry an anchor (the anchor token sits outside the range slice)",
   469	  tag: "{path} must not carry a tag (probe P22: a tag can flip the resolved type silently)",
   470	  form: "{path} must be written as a plain decimal integer >= 1; got source form {source}",
   471	  resolved: "{path} must resolve to a safe integer >= 1",
   472	} as const;
   473	
   474	export function sourceLadderFinding(node: unknown, source: string, path: string): string | undefined {
   475	  if (node === undefined || node === null) return undefined;
   476	  if (isAlias(node)) return render(SOURCE_LADDER.alias, { path });
   477	  if (!isScalar(node)) return render(SOURCE_LADDER.nonScalar, { path });
   478	  if (node.anchor !== undefined) return render(SOURCE_LADDER.anchor, { path });
   479	  if (node.tag !== undefined) return render(SOURCE_LADDER.tag, { path });
   480	  const range = node.range;
   481	  const slice = range ? source.slice(range[0], range[1]) : "";
   482	  if (!PLAIN_DECIMAL.test(slice)) {
   483	    return render(SOURCE_LADDER.form, { path, source: JSON.stringify(slice) });
   484	  }
   485	  const resolved: unknown = node.value;
   486	  if (typeof resolved !== "number" || !Number.isSafeInteger(resolved) || resolved < 1) {
   487	    return render(SOURCE_LADDER.resolved, { path });
   488	  }
   489	  return undefined;
   490	}
   491	
   492	
   493	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   494	// The acyclic-graph lane (the substrate's `resolve.graph`).
   495	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   496	
   497	/** DFS with a PATH-SCOPED ancestor set: shared subtrees (legal anchor
   498	 * reuse) are not cycles; only a back-edge is. */
   499	function findCycle(value: unknown, path: string, ancestors: Set<object>): string | undefined {
   500	  if (typeof value !== "object" || value === null) return undefined;
   501	  if (ancestors.has(value)) return path;
   502	  ancestors.add(value);
   503	  try {
   504	    if (Array.isArray(value)) {
   505	      for (let i = 0; i < value.length; i += 1) {
   506	        const hit = findCycle(value[i], joinPath(path, String(i)), ancestors);
   507	        if (hit !== undefined) return hit;
   508	      }
   509	      return undefined;
   510	    }
   511	    if (isResolvedMap(value)) {
   512	      for (const [key, child] of value.entries()) {
   513	        const segment = typeof key === "string" ? key : "<map-key>";
   514	        const keyHit = findCycle(key, joinPath(path, `${segment}<key>`), ancestors);
   515	        if (keyHit !== undefined) return keyHit;
   516	        const valueHit = findCycle(child, joinPath(path, segment), ancestors);
   517	        if (valueHit !== undefined) return valueHit;
   518	      }
   519	    }
   520	    return undefined;
   521	  } finally {
   522	    ancestors.delete(value);
   523	  }
   524	}
   525	
   526	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   527	// Node evaluation.
   528	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   529	
   530	const DEFAULT_MISSING = 'missing required key "{key}"';
   531	
   532	/** Sibling reliability inside ONE fixed-map evaluation. `dependsOn` reads
   533	 * it before the global map so a defect in binding #1 cannot suppress a
   534	 * lane in binding #2. */
   535	type LocalOk = Map<string, boolean>;
   536	
   537	function baseSlots(frame: Frame): Slots {
   538	  return { path: frame.path, value: frame.value, ownerKey: frame.ownerKey, label: frame.label };
   539	}
   540	
   541	function evaluateNode(run: Run, decl: NodeDecl, frame: Frame, local?: LocalOk): NodeResult {
   542	  const result = dispatch(run, decl, frame, local);
   543	  run.completed.add(frame.decl);
   544	  run.mark(frame.decl, result.ok);
   545	  run.mark(decl.tag, result.ok);
   546	  return result;
   547	}
   548	
   549	function dispatch(run: Run, decl: NodeDecl, frame: Frame, local?: LocalOk): NodeResult {
   550	  switch (decl.kind) {
   551	    case "map.fixed":
   552	      return evalMapFixed(run, decl, frame);
   553	    case "map.open":
   554	      return evalMapOpen(run, decl, frame);
   555	    case "map.plain":
   556	      return evalMapPlain(run, decl, frame);
   557	    case "list":
   558	      return evalList(run, decl, frame, local);
   559	    case "string":
   560	      return evalString(run, decl, frame, local);
   561	    case "integer":
   562	      return evalInteger(run, decl, frame);
   563	    case "enum":
   564	      return evalEnum(run, decl, frame);
   565	    case "union":
   566	      return evalUnion(run, decl, frame);
   567	    case "raw":
   568	      return evalRaw(run, decl, frame);
   569	    case "valueClass":
   570	      return evalValueClassRef(run, decl, frame, local);
   571	    case "delegate":
   572	      return evalDelegate(run, decl, frame, local);
   573	  }
   574	}
   575	
   576	function evalValueClassRef(
   577	  run: Run,
   578	  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "valueClass" }>,
   579	  frame: Frame,
   580	  local?: LocalOk,
   581	): NodeResult {
   582	  const target = run.valueClasses[decl.valueClass];
   583	  if (target === undefined) return { ok: true, value: frame.value };
   584	  const merged: Frame = { ...frame, label: decl.label ?? frame.label };
   585	  const result = dispatch(run, target, merged, local);
   586	  // The REFERENCING site's gating decides, not the shared class's.
   587	  if (!result.ok && decl.gating === true) run.mark(frame.decl, false);
   588	  return result;
   589	}
   590	
   591	function evalMapFixed(
   592	  run: Run,
   593	  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "map.fixed" }>,
   594	  frame: Frame,
   595	): NodeResult {
   596	  const value = frame.value;
   597	  const slots = baseSlots(frame);
   598	  if (!isContainer(value)) {
   599	    run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.containerMessage, slots));
   600	    return { ok: false };
   601	  }
   602	  const container = value;
   603	  const fieldNames = Object.keys(decl.fields);
   604	  // A field declared for ONE channel is legal only there — the same
   605	  // channel scoping D1 applies to source-bearing attributes, applied to
   606	  // keyset membership.
   607	  const inChannel = (name: string): boolean => {
   608	    const channel = decl.fields[name]?.channel;
   609	    return channel === undefined || channel === "both" || channel === run.channel.kind;
   610	  };
   611	  // A channel-scoped field is scoped WHOLE: out of channel it is neither a
   612	  // legal key, nor a missing one, nor evaluated (design review F7).
   613	  const scoped = fieldNames.filter(inChannel);
   614	  const legal = new Set(scoped);
   615	  const normalized: Record<string, unknown> = {};
   616	  const local: LocalOk = new Map();
   617	
   618	  const emitUnknownKeys = (): void => {
   619	    for (const [key] of entriesOf(container)) {
   620	      if (typeof key !== "string") {
   621	        run.emit(
   622	          frame.path,
   623	          render(decl.unknownMessage, { ...slots, key, value: key, keys: scoped.join(", ") }),
   624	        );
   625	        continue;
   626	      }
   627	      if (legal.has(key)) continue;
   628	      const at = joinPath(frame.path, key);
   629	      const removed = ownMessage(decl.removedKeys, key);
   630	      const keySlots: Slots = { ...slots, path: at, key, value: key, keys: scoped.join(", ") };
   631	      run.emit(at, render(removed ?? decl.unknownMessage, keySlots));
   632	    }
   633	  };
   634	
   635	  const emitMissing = (name: string): void => {
   636	    const field = decl.fields[name];
   637	    const presence = field?.presence;
   638	    if (field === undefined || presence?.required !== true) return;
   639	    if (containerHas(container, name)) return;
   640	    if (presence.foldedIntoTypeLane === true) {
   641	      // Absence is this node's OWN type lane (a binding's `uses`).
   642	      evaluateNode(run, field, childFrame(frame, name, undefined, container), local);
   643	      local.set(field.tag, false);
   644	      return;
   645	    }
   646	    const at = presence.at === "self" ? joinPath(frame.path, name) : frame.path;
   647	    run.emit(
   648	      at,
   649	      render(presence.message ?? decl.missingMessage ?? DEFAULT_MISSING, { ...slots, path: at, key: name }),
   650	      presence.code,
   651	    );
   652	    local.set(field.tag, false);
   653	  };
   654	
   655	  const evalOneField = (name: string): void => {
   656	    const field = decl.fields[name];
   657	    if (field === undefined) return;
   658	    if (!containerHas(container, name)) {
   659	      // An ABSENT field is where a declared `default:` materializes — the
   660	      // schema-side half of ADR-019 D3.
   661	      if (field.default !== undefined) defineOwn(normalized, name, field.default);
   662	      return;
   663	    }
   664	    const child = childFrame(frame, name, containerGet(container, name), container);
   665	    const result = evaluateNode(run, field, child, local);
   666	    local.set(field.tag, result.ok);
   667	    if (result.ok && result.value !== undefined) defineOwn(normalized, name, result.value);
   668	  };
   669	
   670	  const order = decl.laneOrder ?? "unknownThenPerKey";
   671	  if (order === "missingThenUnknown") {
   672	    for (const name of scoped) emitMissing(name);
   673	    emitUnknownKeys();
   674	    for (const name of scoped) evalOneField(name);
   675	  } else if (order === "unknownThenMissingThenValues") {
   676	    emitUnknownKeys();
   677	    for (const name of scoped) emitMissing(name);
   678	    for (const name of scoped) evalOneField(name);
   679	  } else {
   680	    emitUnknownKeys();
   681	    for (const name of scoped) {
   682	      if (!containerHas(container, name)) {
   683	        emitMissing(name);
   684	        evalOneField(name);
   685	        continue;
   686	      }
   687	      evalOneField(name);
   688	    }
   689	  }
   690	  return { ok: true, value: normalized };
   691	}
   692	
   693	function childFrame(parent: Frame, key: string, value: unknown, parentValue: unknown): Frame {
   694	  return {
   695	    decl: `${parent.decl}.${key}`,
   696	    path: joinPath(parent.path, key),
   697	    segments: [...parent.segments, key],
   698	    value,
   699	    parentValue,
   700	    ownerKey: parent.ownerKey,
   701	  };
   702	}
   703	
   704	function evalMapOpen(
   705	  run: Run,
   706	  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "map.open" }>,
   707	  frame: Frame,
   708	): NodeResult {
   709	  const value = frame.value;
   710	  const slots = baseSlots(frame);
   711	  if (!isContainer(value)) {
   712	    run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.containerMessage, slots));
   713	    return { ok: false };
   714	  }
   715	  const container = value;
   716	  const entries = entriesOf(container);
   717	  let ok = true;
   718	  if (decl.nonempty !== undefined && entries.length === 0) {
   719	    run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.nonempty.message, slots));
   720	    if (decl.nonempty.gating === true) ok = false;
   721	  }
   722	  if (decl.deepKeyStringness?.channel === run.channel.kind) {
   723	    scanKeyStringness(run, container, frame.path, decl.deepKeyStringness.message, new WeakSet());
   724	  }
   725	  const normalized: Record<string, unknown> = {};
   726	  for (const [key, child] of entries) {
   727	    const keyIsString = typeof key === "string";
   728	    const entryPath = keyIsString ? joinPath(frame.path, key) : frame.path;
   729	    if (decl.keyClass !== undefined) {
   730	      const keyFrame: Frame = {
   731	        decl: `${frame.decl}<key>`,
   732	        path: decl.keyLaneAt === "container" ? frame.path : entryPath,
   733	        segments: frame.segments,
   734	        value: key,
   735	        parentValue: container,
   736	        ownerKey: frame.ownerKey,
   737	      };
   738	      const keyResult = evaluateNode(run, decl.keyClass, keyFrame);
   739	      if (!keyResult.ok && decl.keyClass.gating === true) ok = false;
   740	    }
   741	    if (decl.keysSubsetOf !== undefined) {
   742	      const before = run.findings.length;
   743	      // The rule is the OPEN MAP's, so a `^`-relative selector resolves
   744	      // from the map's own container — never from the map itself.
   745	      const subsetFrame: Frame = {
   746	        decl: frame.decl,
   747	        path: entryPath,
   748	        segments: frame.segments,
   749	        value: key,
   750	        parentValue: frame.parentValue,
   751	        ownerKey: frame.ownerKey,
   752	      };
   753	      checkMembership(run, subsetFrame, decl.keysSubsetOf, key, {
   754	        ...slots,
   755	        path: entryPath,
   756	        key,
   757	        ownerKey: frame.ownerKey,
   758	      });
   759	      // A key outside the subset is DEAD config: nothing below it has an
   760	      // operand to be validated against (the measured lane's CONTINUE).
   761	      if (run.findings.length > before) continue;
   762	    }
   763	    const entryFrame: Frame = {
   764	      decl: `${frame.decl}.*`,
   765	      path: entryPath,
   766	      segments: keyIsString ? [...frame.segments, key] : frame.segments,
   767	      value: child,
   768	      parentValue: container,
   769	      ownerKey: keyIsString ? key : frame.ownerKey,
   770	    };
   771	    const entryResult = evaluateNode(run, decl.entry, entryFrame);
   772	    if (keyIsString && entryResult.ok && entryResult.value !== undefined) {
   773	      defineOwn(normalized, key, entryResult.value);
   774	    }
   775	  }
   776	  run.completed.add(`${frame.decl}.*`);
   777	  return { ok, value: normalized };
   778	}
   779	
   780	/** The gates subtree's file-channel key-STRINGNESS scan: a non-string key
   781	 * cannot become an own property and would blind every own-key scan, so it
   782	 * reports at the NEAREST addressable path (the containing map's). */
   783	function scanKeyStringness(
   784	  run: Run,
   785	  value: unknown,
   786	  path: string,
   787	  message: MessageTemplate,
   788	  seen: WeakSet<object>,
   789	): void {
   790	  if (typeof value !== "object" || value === null) return;
   791	  if (seen.has(value)) return;
   792	  seen.add(value);
   793	  if (Array.isArray(value)) {
   794	    value.forEach((item, index) => scanKeyStringness(run, item, indexPath(path, index), message, seen));
   795	    return;
   796	  }
   797	  if (!isResolvedMap(value)) return;
   798	  for (const [key, child] of value.entries()) {
   799	    if (typeof key !== "string") {
   800	      run.emit(path, render(message, { path, key, value: key }));
   801	      continue;
   802	    }
   803	    scanKeyStringness(run, child, joinPath(path, key), message, seen);
   804	  }
   805	}
   806	
   807	function evalList(
   808	  run: Run,
   809	  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "list" }>,
   810	  frame: Frame,
   811	  local?: LocalOk,
   812	): NodeResult {
   813	  const value = frame.value;
   814	  const slots = baseSlots(frame);
   815	  if (!Array.isArray(value)) {
   816	    run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.containerMessage, slots));
   817	    return { ok: false };
   818	  }
   819	  let ok = true;
   820	  if (decl.nonempty !== undefined && value.length === 0) {
   821	    run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.nonempty.message, slots));
   822	    if (decl.nonempty.gating === true) ok = false;
   823	  }
   824	  const normalized: unknown[] = [];
   825	  const seen = new Set<unknown>();
   826	  const clean: { readonly member: unknown; readonly path: string }[] = [];
   827	  value.forEach((member, index) => {
   828	    const memberPath = decl.memberLaneAt === "container" ? frame.path : indexPath(frame.path, index);
   829	    const memberFrame: Frame = {
   830	      decl: `${frame.decl}[]`,
   831	      path: memberPath,
   832	      segments: [...frame.segments, index],
   833	      value: member,
   834	      parentValue: value,
   835	      ownerKey: frame.ownerKey,
   836	    };
   837	    const before = run.findings.length;
   838	    const result = evaluateNode(run, decl.member, memberFrame, local);
   839	    if (run.findings.length > before) return;
   840	    if (result.value !== undefined) normalized.push(result.value);
   841	    if (decl.memberOf !== undefined) {
   842	      checkMembership(run, memberFrame, decl.memberOf, member, { ...slots, path: memberPath, value: member });
   843	    }
   844	    if (decl.unique?.grain === "perOccurrence") {
   845	      const at = decl.unique.at === "container" ? frame.path : memberPath;
   846	      if (seen.has(member)) {
   847	        run.emit(at, render(decl.unique.message, { ...slots, path: at, value: member }));
   848	      }
   849	      seen.add(member);
   850	    }
   851	    clean.push({ member, path: memberPath });
   852	  });
   853	  if (decl.disjointFrom !== undefined) {
   854	    for (const entry of clean) {
   855	      const memberFrame: Frame = {
   856	        decl: `${frame.decl}[]`,
   857	        path: entry.path,
   858	        segments: frame.segments,
   859	        value: entry.member,
   860	        parentValue: value,
   861	        ownerKey: frame.ownerKey,
   862	      };
   863	      checkMembership(run, memberFrame, decl.disjointFrom, entry.member, {
   864	        ...slots,
   865	        path: entry.path,
   866	        value: entry.member,
   867	      });
   868	    }
   869	  }
   870	  run.completed.add(`${frame.decl}[]`);
   871	  return { ok, value: normalized };
   872	}
   873
