import { parseDocument } from "yaml";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

import type { GateCatalog, GateRegistration } from "../../ports/index.js";
import type { ValidationFinding } from "../errors.js";
import { runSurface } from "./engine.js";
import type { EngineChannel } from "./engine.js";
import { closureProblems, defineSurface, SurfaceDeclarationError } from "./defineSurface.js";
import { normalize } from "./normalizer.js";
import { templateFormat } from "./templateFormat.js";
import type { NodeDecl, SurfaceDecl } from "./vocabulary.js";

/**
 * The ENGINE's own suite (ADR-019's P3 build). It tests the VOCABULARY's
 * semantics over SYNTHETIC declarations — never the template
 * declaration, whose correctness is measured by the 1830-case corpus the
 * parity gate replayed.
 *
 * SENSITIVITY DISCIPLINE (process-log 2026-08-04, the non-discriminating
 * fixture and the vacuous mutant): every guard below carries BOTH its
 * fixture and a MUTANT declaration with that guard removed, and the
 * fixture must stop producing the finding against the mutant. A fixture
 * that fires either way proves nothing.
 *
 * Declaration-as-data makes the mutant cheap and exact: it is the same
 * object with one attribute deleted, not a hand-edited copy of an
 * engine. That is a property of the direction, and it is why this suite
 * can afford a mutant per guard.
 *
 * The register is ONE level: a per-claim fixture, the name list, and a
 * pinned count. No register tower (the documented anti-precedent).
 */

const NO_CATALOG: GateCatalog = { resolve: () => null };

function surface(root: NodeDecl, extra: Partial<SurfaceDecl> = {}): SurfaceDecl {
  return {
    substrate: templateFormat.substrate,
    root,
    valueClasses: {},
    crossRules: [],
    normalizers: [],
    ...extra,
  };
}

function direct(
  root: NodeDecl,
  value: unknown,
  extra?: Partial<SurfaceDecl>,
  catalog: GateCatalog = NO_CATALOG,
): readonly ValidationFinding[] {
  return runSurface(surface(root, extra), value, { channel: { kind: "direct" }, catalog }).findings;
}

function fromFile(
  root: NodeDecl,
  yaml: string,
  extra?: Partial<SurfaceDecl>,
): readonly ValidationFinding[] {
  const doc = parseDocument(yaml);
  const value: unknown = doc.toJS({ mapAsMap: true });
  const channel: EngineChannel = { kind: "file", doc, source: yaml };
  return runSurface(surface(root, extra), value, { channel, catalog: NO_CATALOG }).findings;
}

/** Building blocks kept tiny so a mutant is visibly ONE attribute away. */
const ROWS = ["synthetic"] as const;

function fixed(tag: string, fields: Record<string, NodeDecl>, over: Partial<NodeDecl> = {}): NodeDecl {
  return {
    kind: "map.fixed",
    tag,
    rows: ROWS,
    containerMessage: "{path} must be a map",
    unknownMessage: "unknown key {value}",
    fields,
    ...over,
  } as NodeDecl;
}

function text(tag: string, over: Partial<NodeDecl> = {}): NodeDecl {
  return { kind: "string", tag, rows: ROWS, typeMessage: "{path} must be a string", ...over } as NodeDecl;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// The guard register.
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

interface Guard {
  readonly claim: string;
  readonly decl: NodeDecl;
  /** The SAME declaration with this guard removed. */
  readonly mutant: NodeDecl;
  readonly value?: unknown;
  readonly yaml?: string;
  readonly expected: ValidationFinding;
  readonly extra?: Partial<SurfaceDecl>;
  readonly catalog?: GateCatalog;
}

const idish = { kind: "string", tag: "member", rows: ROWS, grammar: { re: "^[a-z]+$", message: "{path}: bad id" } } as NodeDecl;

const GUARDS: readonly Guard[] = [
  {
    claim: "map.fixed — the container kind",
    decl: fixed("m", {}),
    mutant: { kind: "raw", tag: "m", rows: ROWS },
    value: 7,
    expected: { path: "$", message: "$ must be a map" },
  },
  {
    claim: "map.fixed — a missing required key at CONTAINER grain",
    decl: fixed("m", { a: text("a", { presence: { required: true } }) }, { missingMessage: 'missing required key "{key}"' }),
    mutant: fixed("m", { a: text("a") }, { missingMessage: 'missing required key "{key}"' }),
    value: {},
    expected: { path: "$", message: 'missing required key "a"' },
  },
  {
    claim: "map.fixed — a missing required key at SELF grain with its own wording and code",
    ]);
  });

  it("a config-relative path of \"\" addresses the config object itself", () => {
    const catalog = catalogOf({ ok: false, findings: [{ path: "", message: "config must be a map" }] });
    expect(direct(binding, { uses: "a.b", config: 3 }, undefined, catalog)).toStrictEqual([
      { path: "config", message: "config must be a map" },
    ]);
  });

  it("the delegation BELT: a failure reported with ZERO findings still blocks", () => {
    const catalog = catalogOf({ ok: false, findings: [] });
    expect(direct(binding, { uses: "a.b", config: {} }, undefined, catalog)).toStrictEqual([
      { path: "config", message: "evaluator 'a.b' reported a config failure without findings" },
    ]);
  });

  it("a failed `uses` SUPPRESSES the hand-off — nothing is resolved against a broken id", () => {
    const catalog = catalogOf({ ok: false, findings: [{ path: "", message: "config must be a map" }] });
    expect(direct(binding, { uses: 9, config: {} }, undefined, catalog)).toStrictEqual([
      { path: "uses", message: "uses must be a string" },
    ]);
  });

  it("an ABSENT config still reaches the registration — presence is the evaluator's business", () => {
    const catalog = catalogOf({ ok: false, findings: [{ path: "", message: "a config is required" }] });
    expect(direct(binding, { uses: "a.b" }, undefined, catalog)).toStrictEqual([
      { path: "config", message: "a config is required" },
    ]);
  });
});

describe("the normalizer (ADR-019 D3) — derivation, never validation", () => {
  it("expands a COMPLETE per-edge flag map, all-false when no advancing set is declared", () => {
    const value = {
      steps: {
        a: { transitions: { GO: "b" } },
        b: { transitions: {} },
      },
    };
    normalize(templateFormat, value, new Map());
    expect((value.steps.a as Record<string, unknown>)["advancesRound"]).toStrictEqual({ GO: false });
    expect((value.steps.b as Record<string, unknown>)["advancesRound"]).toStrictEqual({});
  });

  it("expands against the DECLARED advancing set", () => {
    const value = {
      round: { advanceOnArrivalAt: ["b"] },
      steps: { a: { transitions: { GO: "b", STAY: "a" } } },
    };
    normalize(templateFormat, value, new Map());
    expect((value.steps.a as Record<string, unknown>)["advancesRound"]).toStrictEqual({ GO: true, STAY: false });
  });

  it("PRODUCER MONOPOLY: a pre-populated flag map is recomputed, never trusted", () => {
    const value = { steps: { a: { transitions: { GO: "b" }, advancesRound: { GO: true, PHANTOM: true } } } };
    normalize(templateFormat, value, new Map());
    expect((value.steps.a as Record<string, unknown>)["advancesRound"]).toStrictEqual({ GO: false });
  });

  it("writes each registration's EFFECTIVE config into the binding's single config surface", () => {
    const value = {
      steps: { a: { transitions: { GO: "b" }, gates: { GO: [{ uses: "x.y", config: { authored: 1 } }] } } },
    };
    const effective = new Map<string, unknown>([["steps.a.gates.GO[0]", { resolved: 2 }]]);
    normalize(templateFormat, value, effective);
    expect((value.steps.a.gates as Record<string, unknown>)["GO"]).toStrictEqual([
      { uses: "x.y", config: { resolved: 2 } },
    ]);
  });
});

    // One level: the fixture list above IS the register. The pin makes
    // adding or dropping a guard a visible edit rather than a drift.
    expect(GUARDS).toHaveLength(31);
    expect(new Set(GUARDS.map((guard) => guard.claim)).size).toBe(GUARDS.length);
    for (const guard of GUARDS) {
      expect(guard.decl).not.toStrictEqual(guard.mutant);

describe("lane order is DECLARED, because the measured nodes disagree", () => {
  const node = (order: "missingThenUnknown" | "unknownThenPerKey" | "unknownThenMissingThenValues"): NodeDecl =>
    fixed(
      "m",
      {
        a: text("a", { presence: { required: true }, grammar: { re: "^ok$", message: "a bad" } }),
        b: text("b", { presence: { required: true } }),
      },
      { laneOrder: order, missingMessage: 'missing "{key}"' },
    );
  const value = { a: "no", surplus: 1 };

  it("missingThenUnknown — every missing key, then every unknown key, then the value lanes", () => {
    expect(direct(node("missingThenUnknown"), value).map((f) => f.message)).toStrictEqual([
      'missing "b"',
      'unknown key "surplus"',
      "a bad",
    ]);
  });

  it("unknownThenPerKey — unknown keys, then each key's missing-or-value lane in declared order", () => {
    expect(direct(node("unknownThenPerKey"), value).map((f) => f.message)).toStrictEqual([
      'unknown key "surplus"',
      "a bad",
      'missing "b"',
    ]);
  });

  it("unknownThenMissingThenValues — unknown keys, then every missing key, then the value lanes", () => {
    expect(direct(node("unknownThenMissingThenValues"), value).map((f) => f.message)).toStrictEqual([
      'unknown key "surplus"',
      'missing "b"',
      "a bad",
    ]);
  });
});

   366	
   367	/** The node a declaration path addresses, following a value-class
   368	 * reference to the class it names — the node a selector actually meets. */
   369	function nodeAt(path: string, ctx: Context): NodeDecl | undefined {
   370	  const node = ctx.selectorPositions.get(path);
   371	  if (node?.kind !== "valueClass") return node;
   372	  return ctx.valueClasses[node.valueClass];
   373	}
   374	
   375	/**
   376	 * The RUNTIME shapes a declared node can legally hold. A selector reads
   377	 * the RAW value, so what decides whether a relation can read a target is
   378	 * not the node's kind name but the shapes a valid value can take — and a
   379	 * node that can hold more than one shape is readable only for SOME
   380	 * inputs, which is the input-dependent silence the gate exists to refuse.
   381	 */
   382	type RuntimeShape = "map" | "list" | "string" | "other";
   383	
   384	function shapesOf(node: NodeDecl, ctx: Context, seen = new Set<string>()): ReadonlySet<RuntimeShape> {
   385	  switch (node.kind) {
   386	    case "map.fixed":
   387	    case "map.open":
   388	    case "map.plain":
   389	      return new Set<RuntimeShape>(["map"]);
   390	    case "list":
   391	      return new Set<RuntimeShape>(["list"]);
   392	    case "string":
   393	      return new Set<RuntimeShape>(["string"]);
   394	    case "integer":
   395	    case "delegate":
   396	      return new Set<RuntimeShape>(["other"]);
   397	    case "enum":
   398	      return new Set<RuntimeShape>(
   399	        node.members.map((member) => (typeof member.value === "string" ? "string" : "other")),
   400	      );
   401	    case "union": {
   402	      const shapes = new Set<RuntimeShape>((node.literals ?? []).map(() => "string" as const));
   403	      if (node.mapCase !== undefined) for (const shape of shapesOf(node.mapCase, ctx, seen)) shapes.add(shape);
   404	      return shapes;
   405	    }
   406	    case "valueClass": {
   407	      if (seen.has(node.valueClass)) return new Set<RuntimeShape>(["other"]);
   408	      seen.add(node.valueClass);
   409	      const target = ctx.valueClasses[node.valueClass];
   410	      return target === undefined ? new Set<RuntimeShape>(["other"]) : shapesOf(target, ctx, seen);
   411	    }
   412	    case "raw":
   413	      // Uninterpreted: the declaration guarantees nothing about the value.
   414	      return new Set<RuntimeShape>(["map", "list", "string", "other"]);
   415	  }
   416	}
   417	
   418	/** Pass two for selectors: does the target POSITION exist, and is its kind
   419	 * one the relation can read? A syntactically perfect path to a node that
   420	 * does not exist made the rule vanish without a word. */
   421	function resolveSelectors(ctx: Context): void {
   422	  for (const ref of ctx.pending) {
   423	    const segments = ref.path.split(".");
   424	    const head = segments.shift();
   425	    const base = head === "$" ? "$" : ref.from.split(".").slice(0, -1).join(".");
   426	    const target = segments.length === 0 ? base : `${base}.${segments.join(".")}`;
   427	    const node = nodeAt(target, ctx);
   428	    if (node === undefined) {
   429	      ctx.problems.push(
   430	        `${ref.where}: selector path ${JSON.stringify(ref.path)} addresses ${JSON.stringify(target)}, ` +
   431	          `which the declaration does not declare`,
   432	      );
   433	      continue;
   434	    }
   435	    const needed: RuntimeShape = ref.relation === "keysOf" ? "map" : ref.relation === "valuesOf" ? "list" : "string";
   436	    const shapes = shapesOf(node, ctx);
   437	    // EVERY legal value must be readable. One that is readable for some
   438	    // inputs and silently skipped for others is what "closed" must not
   439	    // mean.
   440	    const unreadable = [...shapes].filter((shape) => shape !== needed);
   441	    if (unreadable.length > 0) {
   442	      ctx.problems.push(
   443	        `${ref.where}: ${ref.relation}(${JSON.stringify(ref.path)}) addresses a ${node.kind} whose value can be ` +
   444	          `${[...shapes].sort().join(" | ")}; ${ref.relation} reads only ${needed}, so the rule would be ` +
   445	          `skipped for ${unreadable.sort().join(" | ")} values`,
   446	      );
   447	    }
   448	  }
   449	}
   450	
   451	/** Pass two for the normalizer: its operand paths address declared
   452	 * positions, and its dynamic FIELD names are fields of the node it lands
   453	 * on. An unresolved `edges` produced an empty flag map in silence. */
   454	function resolveHooks(surface: SurfaceDecl, ctx: Context): void {
   455	  for (const hook of surface.normalizers) {
   456	    if (!ctx.resolvableHooks.has(hook.tag)) continue;
   457	    const where = `normalizer "${hook.tag}"`;
   458	    const over = ctx.positions.get(hook.over);
   459	    if (over === undefined) {
   460	      ctx.problems.push(`${where}.over: ${JSON.stringify(hook.over)} is not a declared position`);
   461	      continue;
   462	    }
   463	    if (over.kind !== "map.open") {
   464	      ctx.problems.push(`${where}.over: ${JSON.stringify(hook.over)} is a ${over.kind}; the hook walks a map.open`);
   465	      continue;
   466	    }
   467	    const fieldsOf = (path: string): { readonly node: NodeDecl | undefined; readonly names: readonly string[] } => {
   468	      const node = ctx.positions.get(path);
   469	      return { node, names: node?.kind === "map.fixed" ? Object.keys(node.fields) : [] };
   470	    };
   471	    if (hook.hook === "expandAdvancesRound") {
   472	      if (ctx.positions.get(hook.advanceSet) === undefined) {
   473	        ctx.problems.push(
   474	          `${where}.advanceSet: ${JSON.stringify(hook.advanceSet)} is not a declared position`,
   475	        );
   475	        );
   476	      }
   477	      const entry = fieldsOf(`${hook.over}.*`);
   478	      for (const [label, name] of [["edges", hook.edges], ["into", hook.into]] as const) {
   479	        if (!entry.names.includes(name)) {
   480	          ctx.problems.push(
   481	            `${where}.${label}: ${JSON.stringify(name)} is not a field of ${JSON.stringify(`${hook.over}.*`)} ` +
   482	              `(fields: ${entry.names.join(", ")})`,
   483	          );
   484	        }
   485	      }
   486	      continue;
   487	    }
   488	    const member = fieldsOf(`${hook.over}.*[]`);
   489	    for (const name of [...hook.carry, hook.into]) {
   490	      if (!member.names.includes(name)) {
   491	        ctx.problems.push(
   492	          `${where}: ${JSON.stringify(name)} is not a field of ${JSON.stringify(`${hook.over}.*[]`)} ` +
   493	            `(fields: ${member.names.join(", ")})`,
   494	        );
   495	      }
   496	    }
   497	  }
   498	}
   499	
   500	function checkBranch(
   501	  branch: SubstrateBranch,
   502	  where: string,
   503	  ctx: Context,
   504	  messages: readonly MessageTemplate[] = [],
   505	): void {
   506	  ctx.tags.push(branch.tag);
   507	  if (branch.rows.length === 0) ctx.problems.push(`${where}: cites no ratified row`);
   508	  for (const message of messages) ctx.problems.push(...slotProblems(message, where));
   509	}
   510	
   511	/** Every `dependsOn` tag any rule names, with the site that named it. */
   512	function collectDependsOn(decl: NodeDecl, where: string, into: { tag: string; where: string }[]): void {
   513	  const add = (tags: readonly string[] | undefined, site: string): void => {
   514	    for (const tag of tags ?? []) into.push({ tag, where: site });
   515	  };
   516	  switch (decl.kind) {
   517	    case "map.fixed":
   518	      for (const [name, field] of Object.entries(decl.fields)) collectDependsOn(field, `${where}.${name}`, into);
   519	      return;
   520	    case "map.open":
   521	      add(decl.keysSubsetOf?.dependsOn, `${where}.keysSubsetOf`);
   522	      if (decl.keyClass !== undefined) collectDependsOn(decl.keyClass, `${where}<key>`, into);
   523	      collectDependsOn(decl.entry, `${where}.*`, into);
   524	      return;
   525	    case "list":
   526	      add(decl.memberOf?.dependsOn, `${where}.memberOf`);
   527	      add(decl.disjointFrom?.dependsOn, `${where}.disjointFrom`);
   528	      collectDependsOn(decl.member, `${where}[]`, into);
   529	      return;
   530	    case "string":
   531	      add(decl.memberOf?.dependsOn, `${where}.memberOf`);
   532	      return;
   533	    case "union":
   534	      if (decl.mapCase !== undefined) collectDependsOn(decl.mapCase, where, into);
   535	      return;
   536	    case "delegate":
   537	      add(decl.dependsOn, where);
   538	      return;
   539	    default:
   540	      return;
   541	  }
   542	}
   543	
   544	/**
   545	 * Every unresolved reference in a declaration, as a list of problems.
   546	 * Exported so the guard's own fixtures can assert WHICH reference failed
   547	 * rather than only that something did.
   548	 */
   549	export function closureProblems(surface: SurfaceDecl): readonly string[] {
   550	  const ctx: Context = {
   551	    valueClasses: surface.valueClasses,
   552	    codes: new Set(surface.substrate.codes.values),
   553	    tags: [],
   554	    nodeTags: new Set(),
   555	    positions: new Map(),
   556	    selectorPositions: new Map(),
   557	    pending: [],
   558	    resolvableHooks: new Set(),
   559	    problems: [],
   560	  };
   561	
   562	  const substrate = surface.substrate;
   563	  checkBranch(substrate.read, "substrate.read", ctx, [substrate.read.message]);
   564	  checkBranch(substrate.parse.directive, "substrate.parse.directive", ctx, [substrate.parse.directive.message]);
   565	  checkBranch(substrate.parse.duplicateKeys, "substrate.parse.duplicateKeys", ctx, [
   566	    substrate.parse.duplicateKeys.message,
   567	  ]);
   568	  checkBranch(substrate.resolve.graph, "substrate.resolve.graph", ctx, [substrate.resolve.graph.message]);
   569	  checkBranch(substrate.codes, "substrate.codes", ctx);
   570	  checkBranch(substrate.internalFailure, "substrate.internalFailure", ctx, [substrate.internalFailure.message]);
   571	
   572	  checkNode(surface.root, "$", false, ctx);
   573	  for (const [name, decl] of Object.entries(surface.valueClasses)) {
   574	    checkNode(decl, `valueClass "${name}"`, false, ctx);
   575	    // A value-class definition's tag is never STATUSED by the engine (the
   576	    // reference's tag is), so it must not count as a dependsOn target.
   577	    ctx.nodeTags.delete(decl.tag);
   578	  }
   579	  for (const rule of surface.crossRules) checkCrossRule(rule, ctx);
   580	  for (const hook of surface.normalizers) checkHook(hook, ctx);

    49	/** The placeholder slots a message or a finding-path template may use —
    50	 * the engine's closed set (`renderMessage`). */
    51	const SLOTS = new Set([
    52	  "path",
    53	  "key",
    54	  "keyJson",
    55	  "value",
    56	  "valueRaw",
    57	  "valueJson",
    58	  "source",

    66	const SELECTOR_HEADS = new Set(["$", "^"]);
    67	
    68	function slotProblems(template: MessageTemplate, where: string): string[] {
    69	  const problems: string[] = [];
    70	  for (const match of template.matchAll(/\{(\w+)\}/gu)) {
    71	    const slot = match[1] ?? "";
    72	    if (!SLOTS.has(slot)) {
    73	      problems.push(`${where}: unknown placeholder {${slot}} (the slot set is closed)`);
    74	    }
    75	  }
    76	  return problems;
    77	}
    78	
    79	/** A SELECTOR path: head `$` or `^`, dot-separated nonempty segments, `*`
    80	 * legal only as a whole segment. This is the check that kills the
    81	 * documented-but-inert `..` form — it now throws instead of resolving to
    82	 * nothing. */
    83	function selectorPathProblems(path: string, where: string): string[] {
    84	  const segments = path.split(".");
    85	  const head = segments[0] ?? "";
    86	  if (!SELECTOR_HEADS.has(head)) {
    87	    return [
    88	      `${where}: selector path ${JSON.stringify(path)} must start with "$" (the document root) ` +
    89	        `or "^" (the citing node's container); got ${JSON.stringify(head)}`,
    90	    ];
    91	  }
    92	  const problems: string[] = [];
    93	  for (const segment of segments.slice(1)) {
    94	    if (segment === "") {
    95	      problems.push(`${where}: selector path ${JSON.stringify(path)} has an empty segment`);
    96	    } else if (segment.includes("*") && segment !== "*") {
    97	      problems.push(
    98	        `${where}: selector path ${JSON.stringify(path)} uses "*" inside a segment; ` +
    99	          `"*" is legal only as a whole segment`,
   100	      );
   101	    }
   102	  }
   103	  return problems;
   104	}
   105	
   106	/** A NORMALIZER operand path: `$`-rooted, dot-separated, with `*` legal as
   107	 * a whole segment (the `.*.` wildcard the hook resolver splits on). */
   108	function normalizerPathProblems(path: string, where: string): string[] {
   109	  const segments = path.split(".");
   110	  if (segments[0] !== "$") {
   111	    return [`${where}: normalizer path ${JSON.stringify(path)} must start with "$"`];
   112	  }
   113	  const problems: string[] = [];
   114	  for (const segment of segments.slice(1)) {
   115	    if (segment === "") {
   116	      problems.push(`${where}: normalizer path ${JSON.stringify(path)} has an empty segment`);
   117	    } else if (segment.includes("*") && segment !== "*") {
   118	      problems.push(
   119	        `${where}: normalizer path ${JSON.stringify(path)} uses "*" inside a segment; ` +
   120	          `"*" is legal only as a whole segment`,
   121	      );
   122	    }
   123	  }
   124	  return problems;
   125	}
   126	
   127	/** A FINDING-PATH template is RENDERED, never walked: it may carry closed
   128	 * placeholder slots and must not carry selector syntax. */
   129	function findingPathProblems(path: string, where: string): string[] {
   130	  const problems = slotProblems(path, where);

   321	        );

   323	      return;

   326	      ctx.problems.push(...slotProblems(decl.beltMessage, where));

   328	      // typo there makes the engine read `undefined`, skip the registry
   329	      // and treat the delegation as satisfied.

   334	        );
   335	      }
   336	      return;
   337	    }
   338	  }

   340	
   341	function checkCrossRule(rule: EqualsRuleDecl, ctx: Context): void {
   342	  const where = `crossRule "${rule.tag}"`;
   343	  ctx.tags.push(rule.tag);
   344	  if (rule.rows.length === 0) ctx.problems.push(`${where}: cites no ratified row`);
   345	  // A cross rule cites from the ROOT: it has no citing node, so `^` has no
   346	  // operand and every path it writes must be document-rooted.
   347	  ctx.problems.push(...selectorProblems(rule.left, `${where}.left`, "$", ctx));
   348	  ctx.problems.push(...selectorProblems(rule.right, `${where}.right`, "$", ctx));
   349	  ctx.problems.push(...findingPathProblems(rule.missingFromLeft.at, `${where}.missingFromLeft.at`));
   350	  ctx.problems.push(...findingPathProblems(rule.missingFromRight.at, `${where}.missingFromRight.at`));
   351	  ctx.problems.push(...slotProblems(rule.missingFromLeft.message, `${where}.missingFromLeft`));
   352	  ctx.problems.push(...slotProblems(rule.missingFromRight.message, `${where}.missingFromRight`));
   353	}
   354	
   355	function checkHook(hook: NormalizerHookDecl, ctx: Context): void {
   356	  const where = `normalizer "${hook.tag}"`;
   357	  ctx.tags.push(hook.tag);
   358	  if (hook.rows.length === 0) ctx.problems.push(`${where}: cites no ratified row`);
   359	  const pathProblems = [...normalizerPathProblems(hook.over, `${where}.over`)];
   360	  if (hook.hook === "expandAdvancesRound") {
   361	    pathProblems.push(...normalizerPathProblems(hook.advanceSet, `${where}.advanceSet`));
   362	  }
   363	  ctx.problems.push(...pathProblems);
   364	  if (pathProblems.length === 0) ctx.resolvableHooks.add(hook.tag);
   365	}

+import { normalize } from "/tmp/pairflow-b2-build.0yE2KX/definition/schema/normalizer.js";
+import { closureProblems } from "/tmp/pairflow-b2-build.0yE2KX/definition/schema/defineSurface.js";
+import { templateFormat } from "/tmp/pairflow-b2-build.0yE2KX/definition/schema/templateFormat.js";
+
+const substrate = {
+  read: { tag: "read", rows: [], message: "read" },
+  parse: {
+    directive: { tag: "directive", rows: [], only: "1.2", message: "directive" },
+    duplicateKeys: { tag: "dupes", rows: [], message: "dupes" },
+  },
+  resolve: { graph: { tag: "graph", rows: [], message: "cycle" } },
+  codes: { tag: "codes", rows: [], values: [] },
+  internalFailure: { tag: "internal", rows: [], path: "$", message: "internal" },
+};
+const surface = (root, extra = {}) => ({ substrate, root, valueClasses: {}, crossRules: [], normalizers: [], ...extra });
+const direct = (root, value, extra) => runSurface(surface(root, extra), value, { channel: { kind: "direct" } });
+const fixed = (fields, over = {}) => ({
+  kind: "map.fixed", tag: "root", rows: [], containerMessage: "container", unknownMessage: "unknown", fields, ...over,
+});
+const raw = (tag = "raw", over = {}) => ({ kind: "raw", tag, rows: [], ...over });
+const text = (tag = "text", over = {}) => ({ kind: "string", tag, rows: [], typeMessage: "type", ...over });
+const list = (over = {}) => ({ kind: "list", tag: "list", rows: [], containerMessage: "list-kind", memberLaneAt: "index", member: raw(), ...over });
+
+function show(label, value) {
+  console.log(`${label}: ${inspect(value, { depth: 8, sorted: true })}`);
+}
+
+show("grammar-only/non-string", direct({ kind: "string", tag: "s", rows: [], grammar: { re: "^x$", message: "GRAMMAR {path}" } }, 7));
+show("grammar-only/string-mismatch", direct({ kind: "string", tag: "s", rows: [], grammar: { re: "^x$", message: "GRAMMAR {path}" } }, "y"));
+
+show("fixed/present-undefined", direct(fixed({ a: raw("a", { presence: { required: true } }) }), { a: undefined }));
+show("list/undefined-member", direct(list(), [undefined]));
+show("fixed/empty-default", direct(fixed({ a: raw("a", { default: {} }) }), {}));
+show("fixed/undefined-default", direct(fixed({ a: raw("a", { default: undefined }) }), {}));
+
+const yaml = "v: 5\n";
+const doc = parseDocument(yaml);
+const integerRoot = fixed({ v: { kind: "integer", tag: "v", rows: [], sourceForm: "plainDecimalInteger", resolvedForm: { safeInteger: true, min: 10, message: "MIN10 {path}" } } });
+show("integer/file/source+resolved", runSurface(surface(integerRoot), doc.toJS({ mapAsMap: true }), { channel: { kind: "file", doc, source: yaml } }));
+show("integer/direct/source+resolved", direct(integerRoot, { v: 5 }));
+show("integer/bare-string", direct({ kind: "integer", tag: "i", rows: [] }, "not-an-integer"));
+
+show("unique/index + memberLaneAt=container", direct(list({ memberLaneAt: "container", unique: { grain: "perOccurrence", at: "index", message: "DUP {path}" } }), ["x", "x"]));
+show("unique/index + memberLaneAt=index", direct(list({ memberLaneAt: "index", unique: { grain: "perOccurrence", at: "index", message: "DUP {path}" } }), ["x", "x"]));
+
+const membershipRoot = fixed({
+  allowed: list({ tag: "allowed", member: text("allowed-member") }),
+  items: list({ tag: "items", memberLaneAt: "container", member: text("item"), memberOf: { relation: "memberOf", target: { valuesOf: "$.allowed" }, at: "container", message: "MEM {path}" } }),
+});
+show("memberOf/container + memberLaneAt=container", direct(membershipRoot, { allowed: ["a"], items: ["z"] }));
+
+const edgeSurface = surface(raw(), {
+  normalizers: [{ tag: "n", rows: [], hook: "expandAdvancesRound", over: "$.groups.*.steps", edges: "edges", advanceSet: "$.advance", into: "flags" }],
+});
+const edgeValue = { advance: ["b"], groups: { g: { steps: { a: { edges: { GO: "b" } } } } } };
+show("normalizer/two-level-over before", edgeValue);
+show("normalizer/two-level-over result", normalize(edgeSurface, edgeValue, new Map()));
+
+const rr = ["synthetic"];
+const sx = (tag) => ({ kind: "string", tag, rows: rr, typeMessage: "type" });
+const lx = (tag, member) => ({ kind: "list", tag, rows: rr, containerMessage: "list", memberLaneAt: "index", member });
+const ox = (tag, entry) => ({ kind: "map.open", tag, rows: rr, containerMessage: "map", keyLaneAt: "container", entry });
+const fx = (tag, fields) => ({ kind: "map.fixed", tag, rows: rr, containerMessage: "map", unknownMessage: "unknown", fields });
+const legalExpand = {
+  substrate: templateFormat.substrate,
+  root: fx("eroot", {
+    advance: lx("advance", sx("advanceMember")),
+    groups: ox("groups", fx("group", {
+      steps: ox("steps", fx("step", {
+        edges: ox("edges", sx("target")), flags: raw("flags"),
+      })),
+    })),
+  }),
+  valueClasses: {}, crossRules: [],
+  normalizers: [{ tag: "expand", rows: rr, hook: "expandAdvancesRound", over: "$.groups.*.steps", edges: "edges", advanceSet: "$.advance", into: "flags" }],
+};
+const legalExpandValue = { advance: ["b"], groups: { g: { steps: { a: { edges: { GO: "b" } } } } } };
+show("normalizer/expand-wildcard closure", closureProblems(legalExpand));
+show("normalizer/expand-wildcard result", normalize(legalExpand, legalExpandValue, new Map()));
+
+const binding = fx("binding", { uses: raw("uses"), config: raw("config") });
+const legalMaterialize = {
+  substrate: templateFormat.substrate,
+  root: fx("mroot", { teams: ox("teams", fx("team", { steps: ox("msteps", fx("mstep", { gates: ox("gates", lx("pipeline", binding)) })) })) }),
+  valueClasses: {}, crossRules: [],
+  normalizers: [{ tag: "materialize", rows: rr, hook: "materializeEffectiveConfigs", over: "$.teams.*.steps.*.gates", carry: ["uses"], into: "config" }],
+};
+const legalMaterializeValue = { teams: { t: { steps: { s: { gates: { GO: [{ uses: "x", config: { authored: 1 } }] } } } } } };
+show("normalizer/materialize-two-wildcards closure", closureProblems(legalMaterialize));
+show("normalizer/materialize-two-wildcards result", normalize(legalMaterialize, legalMaterializeValue, new Map([["teams.t.steps.s.gates.GO[0]", { effective: 2 }]])));
+
+show("edges/empty-grammar", direct({ kind: "string", tag: "s", rows: [], grammar: { re: "^$", message: "bad" } }, ""));
+show("edges/single-enum", direct({ kind: "enum", tag: "e", rows: [], members: [{ value: "only" }], message: "bad" }, "only"));
+show("edges/single-union", direct({ kind: "union", tag: "u", rows: [], literals: ["only"], message: "bad" }, "only"));
+show("edges/single-list", direct(list({ member: text("m") }), ["x"]));

+import { parseDocument } from "/Users/felho/dev/pairflow/v3/node_modules/yaml/dist/index.js";
+import { runSurface } from "/tmp/pairflow-b2-build.0yE2KX/definition/schema/engine.js";
+import { normalize } from "/tmp/pairflow-b2-build.0yE2KX/definition/schema/normalizer.js";
+import { closureProblems } from "/tmp/pairflow-b2-build.0yE2KX/definition/schema/defineSurface.js";
+import { templateFormat } from "/tmp/pairflow-b2-build.0yE2KX/definition/schema/templateFormat.js";
+
+const substrate = {
+  read: { tag: "read", rows: [], message: "read" },
+  parse: {
+    directive: { tag: "directive", rows: [], only: "1.2", message: "directive" },
+    duplicateKeys: { tag: "dupes", rows: [], message: "dupes" },
+  },
+  resolve: { graph: { tag: "graph", rows: [], message: "cycle" } },
+  codes: { tag: "codes", rows: [], values: [] },
+  internalFailure: { tag: "internal", rows: [], path: "$", message: "internal" },
+};
+const surface = (root, extra = {}) => ({ substrate, root, valueClasses: {}, crossRules: [], normalizers: [], ...extra });
+const direct = (root, value, extra) => runSurface(surface(root, extra), value, { channel: { kind: "direct" } });
+const fixed = (fields, over = {}) => ({
+  kind: "map.fixed", tag: "root", rows: [], containerMessage: "container", unknownMessage: "unknown", fields, ...over,
+});
+const raw = (tag = "raw", over = {}) => ({ kind: "raw", tag, rows: [], ...over });
+const text = (tag = "text", over = {}) => ({ kind: "string", tag, rows: [], typeMessage: "type", ...over });
+const list = (over = {}) => ({ kind: "list", tag: "list", rows: [], containerMessage: "list-kind", memberLaneAt: "index", member: raw(), ...over });
+
+function show(label, value) {
+  console.log(`${label}: ${inspect(value, { depth: 8, sorted: true })}`);
+}
+
+show("grammar-only/non-string", direct({ kind: "string", tag: "s", rows: [], grammar: { re: "^x$", message: "GRAMMAR {path}" } }, 7));
+show("grammar-only/string-mismatch", direct({ kind: "string", tag: "s", rows: [], grammar: { re: "^x$", message: "GRAMMAR {path}" } }, "y"));
+
+show("fixed/present-undefined", direct(fixed({ a: raw("a", { presence: { required: true } }) }), { a: undefined }));
+show("list/undefined-member", direct(list(), [undefined]));
+show("fixed/empty-default", direct(fixed({ a: raw("a", { default: {} }) }), {}));
+show("fixed/undefined-default", direct(fixed({ a: raw("a", { default: undefined }) }), {}));
+
+const yaml = "v: 5\n";
+const doc = parseDocument(yaml);
+const integerRoot = fixed({ v: { kind: "integer", tag: "v", rows: [], sourceForm: "plainDecimalInteger", resolvedForm: { safeInteger: true, min: 10, message: "MIN10 {path}" } } });
+show("integer/file/source+resolved", runSurface(surface(integerRoot), doc.toJS({ mapAsMap: true }), { channel: { kind: "file", doc, source: yaml } }));
+show("integer/direct/source+resolved", direct(integerRoot, { v: 5 }));
+show("integer/bare-string", direct({ kind: "integer", tag: "i", rows: [] }, "not-an-integer"));
+
+show("unique/index + memberLaneAt=container", direct(list({ memberLaneAt: "container", unique: { grain: "perOccurrence", at: "index", message: "DUP {path}" } }), ["x", "x"]));
+show("unique/index + memberLaneAt=index", direct(list({ memberLaneAt: "index", unique: { grain: "perOccurrence", at: "index", message: "DUP {path}" } }), ["x", "x"]));
+
+const membershipRoot = fixed({
+  allowed: list({ tag: "allowed", member: text("allowed-member") }),
+  items: list({ tag: "items", memberLaneAt: "container", member: text("item"), memberOf: { relation: "memberOf", target: { valuesOf: "$.allowed" }, at: "container", message: "MEM {path}" } }),
+});
+show("memberOf/container + memberLaneAt=container", direct(membershipRoot, { allowed: ["a"], items: ["z"] }));
+
+const edgeSurface = surface(raw(), {
+  normalizers: [{ tag: "n", rows: [], hook: "expandAdvancesRound", over: "$.groups.*.steps", edges: "edges", advanceSet: "$.advance", into: "flags" }],
+});
+const edgeValue = { advance: ["b"], groups: { g: { steps: { a: { edges: { GO: "b" } } } } } };
+show("normalizer/two-level-over before", edgeValue);
+show("normalizer/two-level-over result", normalize(edgeSurface, edgeValue, new Map()));
+
+const rr = ["synthetic"];
+const sx = (tag) => ({ kind: "string", tag, rows: rr, typeMessage: "type" });
+const lx = (tag, member) => ({ kind: "list", tag, rows: rr, containerMessage: "list", memberLaneAt: "index", member });
+const ox = (tag, entry) => ({ kind: "map.open", tag, rows: rr, containerMessage: "map", keyLaneAt: "container", entry });
+const fx = (tag, fields) => ({ kind: "map.fixed", tag, rows: rr, containerMessage: "map", unknownMessage: "unknown", fields });
+const legalExpand = {
+  substrate: templateFormat.substrate,
+  root: fx("eroot", {
+    advance: lx("advance", sx("advanceMember")),
+    groups: ox("groups", fx("group", {
+      steps: ox("steps", fx("step", {
+        edges: ox("edges", sx("target")), flags: { kind: "raw", tag: "flags", rows: rr },
+      })),
+    })),
+  }),
+  valueClasses: {}, crossRules: [],
+  normalizers: [{ tag: "expand", rows: rr, hook: "expandAdvancesRound", over: "$.groups.*.steps", edges: "edges", advanceSet: "$.advance", into: "flags" }],
+};
+const legalExpandValue = { advance: ["b"], groups: { g: { steps: { a: { edges: { GO: "b" } } } } } };
+show("normalizer/expand-wildcard closure", closureProblems(legalExpand));
+show("normalizer/expand-wildcard result", normalize(legalExpand, legalExpandValue, new Map()));
+
+const binding = fx("binding", { uses: { kind: "raw", tag: "uses", rows: rr }, config: { kind: "raw", tag: "config", rows: rr } });
+const legalMaterialize = {
+  substrate: templateFormat.substrate,
+  root: fx("mroot", { teams: ox("teams", fx("team", { steps: ox("msteps", fx("mstep", { gates: ox("gates", lx("pipeline", binding)) })) })) }),
+  valueClasses: {}, crossRules: [],
+  normalizers: [{ tag: "materialize", rows: rr, hook: "materializeEffectiveConfigs", over: "$.teams.*.steps.*.gates", carry: ["uses"], into: "config" }],
+};
+const legalMaterializeValue = { teams: { t: { steps: { s: { gates: { GO: [{ uses: "x", config: { authored: 1 } }] } } } } } };
+show("normalizer/materialize-two-wildcards closure", closureProblems(legalMaterialize));
+show("normalizer/materialize-two-wildcards result", normalize(legalMaterialize, legalMaterializeValue, new Map([["teams.t.steps.s.gates.GO[0]", { effective: 2 }]])));
+
+show("edges/empty-grammar", direct({ kind: "string", tag: "s", rows: [], grammar: { re: "^$", message: "bad" } }, ""));
+show("edges/single-enum", direct({ kind: "enum", tag: "e", rows: [], members: [{ value: "only" }], message: "bad" }, "only"));
+show("edges/single-union", direct({ kind: "union", tag: "u", rows: [], literals: ["only"], message: "bad" }, "only"));
+show("edges/single-list", direct(list({ member: text("m") }), ["x"]));

describe("suppression: the implicit container precondition and declared gating", () => {
  const root = fixed("root", {
    steps: {
      kind: "map.open",
      tag: "steps",
      rows: ROWS,
      containerMessage: "steps must be a map",
      keyLaneAt: "container",
      entry: text("step"),
    },
    start: {
      kind: "string",
      tag: "start",
      rows: ROWS,
      memberOf: { relation: "memberOf", target: { keysOf: "$.steps" }, message: "start must name a step" },
    },
  });

  it("a WRONG-KIND container yields its own finding and suppresses the rule that selects over it", () => {
    const findings = direct(root, { steps: 7, start: "nope" });
    expect(findings).toStrictEqual([{ path: "steps", message: "steps must be a map" }]);
  });

  it("a MISSING container suppresses the same rule", () => {
    expect(direct(root, { start: "nope" })).toStrictEqual([]);
  });

  it("an EMPTY container does NOT suppress it — the set exists and is empty", () => {
    expect(direct(root, { steps: {}, start: "nope" })).toStrictEqual([
      { path: "start", message: "start must name a step" },
    ]);
  });

  it("a declared `gating` key class makes the selector's operand unreliable", () => {
    const gated = fixed("root", {
      steps: {
        kind: "map.open",
        tag: "steps",
        rows: ROWS,
        containerMessage: "steps must be a map",
        keyClass: { ...(idish as { kind: "string" }), gating: true } as NodeDecl,
        keyLaneAt: "container",
        entry: text("step"),
      } as NodeDecl,
      start: {
        kind: "string",
        tag: "start",
        rows: ROWS,
        memberOf: { relation: "memberOf", target: { keysOf: "$.steps" }, message: "start must name a step" },
      },
    });
    const messages = direct(gated, { steps: { BAD: "x" }, start: "nope" }).map((f) => f.message);
    expect(messages).toContain("steps: bad id");
    expect(messages).not.toContain("start must name a step");
  });
});

describe("selectors", () => {
  const root = fixed("root", {
    a: { kind: "map.open", tag: "a", rows: ROWS, containerMessage: "c", keyLaneAt: "container", entry: text("ae") },
    b: { kind: "list", tag: "b", rows: ROWS, containerMessage: "c", memberLaneAt: "index", member: text("be") },
    pick: {
      kind: "string",
      tag: "pick",
      rows: ROWS,
      memberOf: {
        relation: "memberOf",
        target: { union: [{ keysOf: "$.a" }, { valuesOf: "$.b" }] },
        message: "pick must be in a or b",
      },
    },
  });

  it("a union of keys(..) and values(..) accepts a member of EITHER", () => {
    expect(direct(root, { a: { x: "1" }, b: ["y"], pick: "y" })).toStrictEqual([]);
    expect(direct(root, { a: { x: "1" }, b: ["y"], pick: "x" })).toStrictEqual([]);
  });

  it("a member of NEITHER is a finding", () => {
    expect(direct(root, { a: { x: "1" }, b: ["y"], pick: "z" })).toStrictEqual([
      { path: "pick", message: "pick must be in a or b" },
    ]);
  });

  it("an operand not yet evaluated is DEFERRED, not read early", () => {
    // `pick` is declared BEFORE `a`, so its operand is pending when the
    // field is reached; a naive engine reads an empty set and reports.
    const early = fixed("root", {
      pick: {
        kind: "string",
        tag: "pick",
        rows: ROWS,
        memberOf: { relation: "memberOf", target: { keysOf: "$.a" }, message: "pick must be in a" },
      },
      a: { kind: "map.open", tag: "a", rows: ROWS, containerMessage: "c", keyLaneAt: "container", entry: text("ae") },
    });
    expect(direct(early, { pick: "x", a: { x: "1" } })).toStrictEqual([]);
    expect(direct(early, { pick: "zz", a: { x: "1" } })).toStrictEqual([
      { path: "pick", message: "pick must be in a" },
    ]);
  });

  it("the `^` root resolves from the citing node's OWN container, never from itself", () => {
    const nested = fixed("root", {
      step: fixed("step", {
        edges: { kind: "map.open", tag: "edges", rows: ROWS, containerMessage: "c", keyLaneAt: "container", entry: text("t") },
        gates: {
          kind: "map.open",
          tag: "gates",
          rows: ROWS,
          containerMessage: "c",
          keyLaneAt: "container",
          keysSubsetOf: { relation: "keysSubsetOf", target: { keysOf: "^.edges" }, message: "dead config: '{key}'" },
          entry: text("g"),
        },
      }),
    });
    expect(direct(nested, { step: { edges: { GO: "x" }, gates: { GO: "g" } } })).toStrictEqual([]);
    expect(direct(nested, { step: { edges: { GO: "x" }, gates: { NOPE: "g" } } })).toStrictEqual([
      { path: "step.gates.NOPE", message: "dead config: 'NOPE'" },
    ]);
  });
});

+show("integer/file/source+resolved", runSurface(surface(integerRoot), doc.toJS({ mapAsMap: true }), { channel: { kind: "file", doc, source: yaml } }));

+show("unique/index + memberLaneAt=index", direct(list({ memberLaneAt: "index", unique: { grain: "perOccurrence", at: "index", message: "DUP {path}" } }), ["x", "x"]));

+  allowed: list({ tag: "allowed", member: text("allowed-member") }),
+  items: list({ tag: "items", memberLaneAt: "container", member: text("item"), memberOf: { relation: "memberOf", target: { valuesOf: "$.allowed" }, at: "container", message: "MEM {path}" } }),
+});

+});

+show("normalizer/two-level-over before", edgeValue);
+show("normalizer/two-level-over result", normalize(edgeSurface, edgeValue, new Map()));

+const lx = (tag, member) => ({ kind: "list", tag, rows: rr, containerMessage: "list", memberLaneAt: "index", member });
+const ox = (tag, entry) => ({ kind: "map.open", tag, rows: rr, containerMessage: "map", keyLaneAt: "container", entry });
+const fx = (tag, fields) => ({ kind: "map.fixed", tag, rows: rr, containerMessage: "map", unknownMessage: "unknown", fields });

+const surface = (root, extra = {}) => ({ substrate, root, valueClasses: {}, crossRules: [], normalizers: [], ...extra });
+const direct = (root, value, extra) => runSurface(surface(root, extra), value, { channel: { kind: "direct" } });
+const fixed = (fields, over = {}) => ({
+  kind: "map.fixed", tag: "root", rows: [], containerMessage: "container", unknownMessage: "unknown", fields, ...over,
+});
+const raw = (tag = "raw", over = {}) => ({ kind: "raw", tag, rows: [], ...over });
+const text = (tag = "text", over = {}) => ({ kind: "string", tag, rows: [], typeMessage: "type", ...over });
+const list = (over = {}) => ({ kind: "list", tag: "list", rows: [], containerMessage: "list-kind", memberLaneAt: "index", member: raw(), ...over });
+
+function show(label, value) {
+  console.log(`${label}: ${inspect(value, { depth: 8, sorted: true })}`);
+}
+
+show("grammar-only/non-string", direct({ kind: "string", tag: "s", rows: [], grammar: { re: "^x$", message: "GRAMMAR {path}" } }, 7));
+show("grammar-only/string-mismatch", direct({ kind: "string", tag: "s", rows: [], grammar: { re: "^x$", message: "GRAMMAR {path}" } }, "y"));
+
+show("fixed/present-undefined", direct(fixed({ a: raw("a", { presence: { required: true } }) }), { a: undefined }));
+show("list/undefined-member", direct(list(), [undefined]));
+show("fixed/empty-default", direct(fixed({ a: raw("a", { default: {} }) }), {}));
+show("fixed/undefined-default", direct(fixed({ a: raw("a", { default: undefined }) }), {}));
+
+const yaml = "v: 5\n";
+const doc = parseDocument(yaml);
+const integerRoot = fixed({ v: { kind: "integer", tag: "v", rows: [], sourceForm: "plainDecimalInteger", resolvedForm: { safeInteger: true, min: 10, message: "MIN10 {path}" } } });
+show("integer/file/source+resolved", runSurface(surface(integerRoot), doc.toJS({ mapAsMap: true }), { channel: { kind: "file", doc, source: yaml } }));
+show("integer/direct/source+resolved", direct(integerRoot, { v: 5 }));
+show("integer/bare-string", direct({ kind: "integer", tag: "i", rows: [] }, "not-an-integer"));
+
+show("unique/index + memberLaneAt=container", direct(list({ memberLaneAt: "container", unique: { grain: "perOccurrence", at: "index", message: "DUP {path}" } }), ["x", "x"]));
+show("unique/index + memberLaneAt=index", direct(list({ memberLaneAt: "index", unique: { grain: "perOccurrence", at: "index", message: "DUP {path}" } }), ["x", "x"]));
+

+  allowed: list({ tag: "allowed", member: text("allowed-member") }),
+  items: list({ tag: "items", memberLaneAt: "container", member: text("item"), memberOf: { relation: "memberOf", target: { valuesOf: "$.allowed" }, at: "container", message: "MEM {path}" } }),
+});
+show("memberOf/container + memberLaneAt=container", direct(membershipRoot, { allowed: ["a"], items: ["z"] }));
+
+const scopedDependsRoot = fixed({
+  entries: {
+    kind: "map.open", tag: "entries", rows: [], containerMessage: "map", keyLaneAt: "container",
+    entry: fixed({
+      guard: { kind: "string", tag: "guard", rows: [], grammar: { re: "^ok$", message: "GUARD {path}" } },
+      pick: { kind: "string", tag: "pick", rows: [], typeMessage: "type", memberOf: { relation: "memberOf", target: { valuesOf: "$.allowed" }, dependsOn: ["guard"], message: "PICK {path}" } },
+    }, { tag: "entry" }),
+  },
+  allowed: list({ tag: "allowed", member: text("allowedMember") }),
+});

+show("normalizer/two-level-over result", normalize(edgeSurface, edgeValue, new Map()));
+

+const sx = (tag) => ({ kind: "string", tag, rows: rr, typeMessage: "type" });
+const lx = (tag, member) => ({ kind: "list", tag, rows: rr, containerMessage: "list", memberLaneAt: "index", member });
+const ox = (tag, entry) => ({ kind: "map.open", tag, rows: rr, containerMessage: "map", keyLaneAt: "container", entry });

+show("edges/empty-grammar", direct({ kind: "string", tag: "s", rows: [], grammar: { re: "^$", message: "bad" } }, ""));
+show("edges/single-enum", direct({ kind: "enum", tag: "e", rows: [], members: [{ value: "only" }], message: "bad" }, "only"));
+show("edges/single-union", direct({ kind: "union", tag: "u", rows: [], literals: ["only"], message: "bad" }, "only"));

codex
A futtatott próbák már több, egymástól független specifikáció–interpreter eltérést igazoltak, köztük egy ismételt open-map bejegyzések között kiszivárgó `dependsOn`-állapotot és mindkét normalizer hook által elfogadott, de nem bejárt wildcard-útvonalakat. Lezárom a feltáró szakaszt; már csak a lefedettségi kontrollt és a repo byte-guardját futtatom, utána a bizonyítékos leletlista következik.

+  normalizers: [{ tag: "materialize", rows: rr, hook: "materializeEffectiveConfigs", over: "$.teams.*.steps.*.gates", carry: ["uses"], into: "config" }],
+};
+const legalMaterializeValue = { teams: { t: { steps: { s: { gates: { GO: [{ uses: "x", config: { authored: 1 } }] } } } } } };
+show("normalizer/materialize-two-wildcards closure", closureProblems(legalMaterialize));
+show("normalizer/materialize-two-wildcards result", normalize(legalMaterialize, legalMaterializeValue, new Map([["teams.t.steps.s.gates.GO[0]", { effective: 2 }]])));
+
+show("edges/empty-grammar", direct({ kind: "string", tag: "s", rows: [], grammar: { re: "^$", message: "bad" } }, ""));
+show("edges/single-enum", direct({ kind: "enum", tag: "e", rows: [], members: [{ value: "only" }], message: "bad" }, "only"));
+show("edges/single-union", direct({ kind: "union", tag: "u", rows: [], literals: ["only"], message: "bad" }, "only"));
+show("edges/single-list", direct(list({ member: text("m") }), ["x"]));

### F1 — A csak `grammar`-t deklaráló string node nem jelent nem-string értéket
CLASS: IN-SCOPE  
LENS: A  
WHAT: A dokumentáció szerint a `grammar.message` lefedi a típus- és grammatikai sávot is, az engine viszont nem-string értéknél finding nélkül tér vissza.  
WHERE: vocabulary.ts:230-238 versus engine.ts:882-899  
EVIDENCE:
```text
node /tmp/pairflow-b2-probes.mjs | sed -n '/grammar-only\/non-string/,/fixed\/present-undefined/p'
grammar-only/non-string: {
  effectiveConfigs: Map(0) {},
  findings: [],
  normalized: undefined,
  runtimeContextBindings: []
}
grammar-only/string-mismatch: {
  effectiveConfigs: Map(0) {},
  findings: [ { message: 'GRAMMAR $', path: '$' } ],
  normalized: undefined,
  runtimeContextBindings: []
}
fixed/present-undefined: {
```
WHY IT MATTERS: Egy deklarációszerző jogosan elhagyhatja a `typeMessage`-et, ekkor viszont a hibás típus csendben, finding nélkül bukik el.  
FIX SKETCH: A kódot javítanám: nem-string értéknél, ha nincs `typeMessage`, de van `grammar`, az engine emittálja a grammar üzenetét.

### F2 — A `raw` pass-through elveszíti a jelen lévő `undefined` értéket
CLASS: IN-SCOPE  
LENS: B  
WHAT: A `raw` node dokumentáltan értelmezés nélküli pass-through, de fixed-map mezőként és listaelemként is eldobja a sikeresen elfogadott `undefined` értéket.  
WHERE: vocabulary.ts:282-287 versus engine.ts:667,840  
EVIDENCE:
```text
node /tmp/pairflow-b2-probes.mjs | sed -n '/fixed\/present-undefined/,/fixed\/empty-default/p'
fixed/present-undefined: {
  effectiveConfigs: Map(0) {},
  findings: [],
  normalized: {},
  runtimeContextBindings: []
}
list/undefined-member: {
  effectiveConfigs: Map(0) {},
  findings: [],
  normalized: [],
  runtimeContextBindings: []
}
fixed/empty-default: {
```
WHY IT MATTERS: A jelen lévő kulcs összekeveredik a hiányzó kulccsal, listában pedig még az indexek is eltolódhatnak az admitted formban.  
FIX SKETCH: A kódot javítanám: a `NodeResult` különböztesse meg a „nincs előállított érték” állapotot a sikeresen előállított `undefined`-tól, és a két konténeríró ezt használja.

### F3 — Az explicit `default: undefined` nem materializálódik
CLASS: IN-SCOPE  
LENS: B  
WHAT: A `default` típusa `unknown`, és a komment minden deklarált plain default materializálását ígéri, az engine azonban `!== undefined` sentinelként kezeli, ezért az explicit undefined default eltűnik.  
WHERE: vocabulary.ts:150-153 versus engine.ts:658-662  
EVIDENCE:
```text
node /tmp/pairflow-b2-probes.mjs | sed -n '/fixed\/empty-default/,/integer\/file/p'
fixed/empty-default: {
  effectiveConfigs: Map(0) {},
  findings: [],
  normalized: { a: {} },
  runtimeContextBindings: []
}
fixed/undefined-default: {
  effectiveConfigs: Map(0) {},
  findings: [],
  normalized: {},
  runtimeContextBindings: []
}
integer/file/source+resolved: {
```
WHY IT MATTERS: A legális default érték jelentése attól függ, hogy egybeesik-e az engine belső hiány-sentinelével.  
FIX SKETCH: A kódot javítanám: `field.default !== undefined` helyett own-property jelenlét alapján kell eldönteni, hogy deklaráltak-e defaultot.

### F4 — File channelon a `sourceForm` kikapcsolja a deklarált `resolvedForm` korlátot
CLASS: IN-SCOPE  
LENS: A  
WHAT: Az integer deklaráció külön source- és resolved-form övet enged egyszerre, de sikeres file-source ellenőrzés után az engine korán visszatér, és nem alkalmazza a deklarált resolved minimumot.  
WHERE: vocabulary.ts:241-252 versus engine.ts:914-929  
EVIDENCE:
```text
node /tmp/pairflow-b2-probes.mjs | sed -n '/integer\/file\/source+resolved/,/integer\/bare-string/p'
integer/file/source+resolved: {
  effectiveConfigs: Map(0) {},
  findings: [],
  normalized: { v: 5 },
  runtimeContextBindings: []
}
integer/direct/source+resolved: {
  effectiveConfigs: Map(0) {},
  findings: [ { message: 'MIN10 v', path: 'v' } ],
  normalized: {},
  runtimeContextBindings: []
}
integer/bare-string: {
```
WHY IT MATTERS: Ugyanaz a deklaráció ugyanazt a feloldott `5` értéket direct channelon elutasítja, file channelon pedig elfogadja, noha a különbség csak a source-form ellenőrzés lenne.  
FIX SKETCH: A kódot javítanám: sikeres source ladder után nem szabad visszatérni; tovább kell futtatni a `resolvedForm` övet is.

### F5 — A `unique.at: "index"` grain felülíródik a listatag grainjével
CLASS: IN-SCOPE  
LENS: C  
WHAT: A `UniqueRule` saját `at: "index"` beállítása index-pathot ígér, de `memberLaneAt: "container"` mellett az engine a listakonténerre címezi a duplikációt.  
WHERE: vocabulary.ts:107-112,222-225 versus engine.ts:827-847  
EVIDENCE:
```text
node /tmp/pairflow-b2-probes.mjs | sed -n '/unique\/index + memberLaneAt=container/,/memberOf\/container/p'
unique/index + memberLaneAt=container: {
  effectiveConfigs: Map(0) {},
  findings: [ { message: 'DUP $', path: '$' } ],
  normalized: [ 'x', 'x' ],
  runtimeContextBindings: []
}
unique/index + memberLaneAt=index: {
  effectiveConfigs: Map(0) {},
  findings: [ { message: 'DUP $[1]', path: '$[1]' } ],
  normalized: [ 'x', 'x' ],
  runtimeContextBindings: []
}
memberOf/container + memberLaneAt=container: {
```
WHY IT MATTERS: Egy új deklaráció szerzője explicit index grain mellett is rossz helyre mutató findingot kap.  
FIX SKETCH: A kódot javítanám: a unique index-pathot közvetlenül `indexPath(frame.path, index)` alapján kell képezni, a `memberLaneAt`-tól függetlenül.

### F6 — A `dependsOn` hibastátusz átszivárog open-map példányok között
CLASS: IN-SCOPE  
LENS: A  
WHAT: Egy bejegyzés dependency-hibája csak annak dependenseit kellene elnyomja, de a tag globális, sticky `false` állapota egy másik, érvényes bejegyzés findingját is elnyomja.  
WHERE: vocabulary.ts:103-104,154-155 versus engine.ts:315-322,429,532-535  
EVIDENCE:
```text
node /tmp/pairflow-b2-probes.mjs | sed -n '/dependsOn\/repeated-entry-scope/,/normalizer\/two-level-over before/p'
dependsOn/repeated-entry-scope: {
  effectiveConfigs: Map(0) {},
  findings: [ { message: 'GUARD entries.bad.guard', path: 'entries.bad.guard' } ],
  normalized: {
    allowed: [ 'a' ],
    entries: { bad: { pick: 'z' }, good: { guard: 'ok', pick: 'z' } }
  },
  runtimeContextBindings: []
}
normalizer/two-level-over before: {
```
WHY IT MATTERS: Egy hibás open-map entry elrejtheti más, független entry-k valódi hibáit.  
FIX SKETCH: A kódot javítanám: ahol van lokális sibling-státusz, annak kell elsőbbséget élveznie a globális tagállapottal szemben; ezt a deferred check során is meg kell őrizni.

### F7 — A normalizer elfogad wildcard útvonalakat, amelyeket egyik hook sem jár be általánosan
CLASS: IN-SCOPE  
LENS: A  
WHAT: A dokumentált normalizer path nyelv egész szegmenses `*` wildcardot enged, és a load gate a próbák útvonalait elfogadja, de az `expandAdvancesRound` literális kulcsként kezeli, a `materializeEffectiveConfigs` pedig csak egy hard-coded wildcard alakot kezel.  
WHERE: vocabulary.ts:69-74,358-382 versus normalizer.ts:38-45,59-62,88-98  
EVIDENCE:
```text
node /tmp/pairflow-b2-probes.mjs | sed -n '/normalizer\/expand-wildcard closure/,/edges\/empty-grammar/p'
normalizer/expand-wildcard closure: []
normalizer/expand-wildcard result: {
  advance: [ 'b' ],
  groups: {
    g: {
      steps: { a: { edges: { GO: 'b' } } }
    }
  }
}
normalizer/materialize-two-wildcards closure: []
normalizer/materialize-two-wildcards result: {
  teams: {
    t: {
      steps: {
        s: {
          gates: {
            GO: [ { config: { authored: 1 }, uses: 'x' } ]
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }
}
edges/empty-grammar: {
```
WHY IT MATTERS: Egy load-time érvényes deklaráció csendben semmit sem normalizál; a későbbi kód hamisan kész admitted formot kap.  
FIX SKETCH: A kódot javítanám: mindkét hook operandusfeloldása járja be minden deklarált `*` szegmenst; a wildcardot ígérő komment és a load gate már ugyanazt a nyelvet képviseli.

WHY IT MATTERS: A deklarációszerző nem tudja, hogy a „DEEP scan” minden hibás kulcsot vagy csak az első elérhetetlen ágat jelenti-e.

+import { inspect } from "node:util";

+const substrate = {
+  read: { tag: "read", rows: [], message: "read" },
+  parse: {
+    directive: { tag: "directive", rows: [], only: "1.2", message: "directive" },
+    duplicateKeys: { tag: "dupes", rows: [], message: "dupes" },
+  },
+  resolve: { graph: { tag: "graph", rows: [], message: "cycle" } },
+  codes: { tag: "codes", rows: [], values: [] },
+  internalFailure: { tag: "internal", rows: [], path: "$", message: "internal" },
+};
+const surface = (root, extra = {}) => ({ substrate, root, valueClasses: {}, crossRules: [], normalizers: [], ...extra });
+const direct = (root, value, extra) => runSurface(surface(root, extra), value, { channel: { kind: "direct" } });
+const fixed = (fields, over = {}) => ({
+  kind: "map.fixed", tag: "root", rows: [], containerMessage: "container", unknownMessage: "unknown", fields, ...over,
+});
+const raw = (tag = "raw", over = {}) => ({ kind: "raw", tag, rows: [], ...over });
+const text = (tag = "text", over = {}) => ({ kind: "string", tag, rows: [], typeMessage: "type", ...over });
+const list = (over = {}) => ({ kind: "list", tag: "list", rows: [], containerMessage: "list-kind", memberLaneAt: "index", member: raw(), ...over });
+
+function show(label, value) {
+  console.log(`${label}: ${inspect(value, { depth: 8, sorted: true })}`);
+}
+
+show("grammar-only/non-string", direct({ kind: "string", tag: "s", rows: [], grammar: { re: "^x$", message: "GRAMMAR {path}" } }, 7));
+show("grammar-only/string-mismatch", direct({ kind: "string", tag: "s", rows: [], grammar: { re: "^x$", message: "GRAMMAR {path}" } }, "y"));
+
+show("fixed/present-undefined", direct(fixed({ a: raw("a", { presence: { required: true } }) }), { a: undefined }));
+show("list/undefined-member", direct(list(), [undefined]));
+show("fixed/empty-default", direct(fixed({ a: raw("a", { default: {} }) }), {}));
+show("fixed/undefined-default", direct(fixed({ a: raw("a", { default: undefined }) }), {}));

+show("integer/bare-string", direct({ kind: "integer", tag: "i", rows: [] }, "not-an-integer"));

+
+const membershipRoot = fixed({
+  allowed: list({ tag: "allowed", member: text("allowed-member") }),

+show("memberOf/container + memberLaneAt=container", direct(membershipRoot, { allowed: ["a"], items: ["z"] }));

+const scopedDependsRoot = fixed({
+  entries: {
+    kind: "map.open", tag: "entries", rows: [], containerMessage: "map", keyLaneAt: "container",
+    entry: fixed({
+      guard: { kind: "string", tag: "guard", rows: [], grammar: { re: "^ok$", message: "GUARD {path}" } },

+show("dependsOn/repeated-entry-scope", direct(scopedDependsRoot, { entries: { bad: { guard: "no", pick: "z" }, good: { guard: "ok", pick: "z" } }, allowed: ["a"] }));
+
+const edgeSurface = surface(raw(), {

+const ox = (tag, entry) => ({ kind: "map.open", tag, rows: rr, containerMessage: "map", keyLaneAt: "container", entry });
+const fx = (tag, fields) => ({ kind: "map.fixed", tag, rows: rr, containerMessage: "map", unknownMessage: "unknown", fields });
+const legalExpand = {

### F4 — File channelon a `sourceForm` kikapcsolja a deklarált `resolvedForm` korlátot
CLASS: IN-SCOPE  
LENS: A  
WHAT: Az integer deklaráció külön source- és resolved-form övet enged egyszerre, de sikeres file-source ellenőrzés után az engine korán visszatér, és nem alkalmazza a deklarált resolved minimumot.  
WHERE: vocabulary.ts:241-252 versus engine.ts:914-929  
EVIDENCE:
```text
node /tmp/pairflow-b2-probes.mjs | sed -n '/integer\/file\/source+resolved/,/integer\/bare-string/p'
integer/file/source+resolved: {
  effectiveConfigs: Map(0) {},
  findings: [],
  normalized: { v: 5 },
  runtimeContextBindings: []
}
integer/direct/source+resolved: {
  effectiveConfigs: Map(0) {},
  findings: [ { message: 'MIN10 v', path: 'v' } ],
  normalized: {},
  runtimeContextBindings: []
}
integer/bare-string: {
```
WHY IT MATTERS: Ugyanaz a deklaráció ugyanazt a feloldott `5` értéket direct channelon elutasítja, file channelon pedig elfogadja, noha a különbség csak a source-form ellenőrzés lenne.  
FIX SKETCH: A kódot javítanám: sikeres source ladder után nem szabad visszatérni; tovább kell futtatni a `resolvedForm` övet is.

### F5 — A `unique.at: "index"` grain felülíródik a listatag grainjével
CLASS: IN-SCOPE  
LENS: C  
WHAT: A `UniqueRule` saját `at: "index"` beállítása index-pathot ígér, de `memberLaneAt: "container"` mellett az engine a listakonténerre címezi a duplikációt.  
WHERE: vocabulary.ts:107-112,222-225 versus engine.ts:827-847  
EVIDENCE:
```text
node /tmp/pairflow-b2-probes.mjs | sed -n '/unique\/index + memberLaneAt=container/,/memberOf\/container/p'
unique/index + memberLaneAt=container: {
  effectiveConfigs: Map(0) {},
  findings: [ { message: 'DUP $', path: '$' } ],
  normalized: [ 'x', 'x' ],
  runtimeContextBindings: []
}
unique/index + memberLaneAt=index: {
  effectiveConfigs: Map(0) {},
  findings: [ { message: 'DUP $[1]', path: '$[1]' } ],
  normalized: [ 'x', 'x' ],
  runtimeContextBindings: []
}
memberOf/container + memberLaneAt=container: {
```
WHY IT MATTERS: Egy új deklaráció szerzője explicit index grain mellett is rossz helyre mutató findingot kap.  
FIX SKETCH: A kódot javítanám: a unique index-pathot közvetlenül `indexPath(frame.path, index)` alapján kell képezni, a `memberLaneAt`-tól függetlenül.

### F6 — A `dependsOn` hibastátusz átszivárog open-map példányok között
CLASS: IN-SCOPE  
LENS: A  
WHAT: Egy bejegyzés dependency-hibája csak annak dependenseit kellene elnyomja, de a tag globális, sticky `false` állapota egy másik, érvényes bejegyzés findingját is elnyomja.  
WHERE: vocabulary.ts:103-104,154-155 versus engine.ts:315-322,429,532-535  
EVIDENCE:
```text
node /tmp/pairflow-b2-probes.mjs | sed -n '/dependsOn\/repeated-entry-scope/,/normalizer\/two-level-over before/p'
dependsOn/repeated-entry-scope: {
  effectiveConfigs: Map(0) {},
  findings: [ { message: 'GUARD entries.bad.guard', path: 'entries.bad.guard' } ],
  normalized: {
    allowed: [ 'a' ],
    entries: { bad: { pick: 'z' }, good: { guard: 'ok', pick: 'z' } }
  },
  runtimeContextBindings: []
}
normalizer/two-level-over before: {
```
WHY IT MATTERS: Egy hibás open-map entry elrejtheti más, független entry-k valódi hibáit.  
FIX SKETCH: A kódot javítanám: ahol van lokális sibling-státusz, annak kell elsőbbséget élveznie a globális tagállapottal szemben; ezt a deferred check során is meg kell őrizni.

### F7 — A normalizer elfogad wildcard útvonalakat, amelyeket egyik hook sem jár be általánosan
CLASS: IN-SCOPE  
LENS: A  
WHAT: A dokumentált normalizer path nyelv egész szegmenses `*` wildcardot enged, és a load gate a próbák útvonalait elfogadja, de az `expandAdvancesRound` literális kulcsként kezeli, a `materializeEffectiveConfigs` pedig csak egy hard-coded wildcard alakot kezel.  
WHERE: vocabulary.ts:69-74,358-382 versus normalizer.ts:38-45,59-62,88-98  
EVIDENCE:
```text
node /tmp/pairflow-b2-probes.mjs | sed -n '/normalizer\/expand-wildcard closure/,/edges\/empty-grammar/p'
normalizer/expand-wildcard closure: []
normalizer/expand-wildcard result: {
  advance: [ 'b' ],
  groups: {
    g: {
      steps: { a: { edges: { GO: 'b' } } }
    }
  }
}
normalizer/materialize-two-wildcards closure: []
normalizer/materialize-two-wildcards result: {
  teams: {
    t: {
      steps: {
        s: {
          gates: {
            GO: [ { config: { authored: 1 }, uses: 'x' } ]
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }
}
edges/empty-grammar: {
```
WHY IT MATTERS: Egy load-time érvényes deklaráció csendben semmit sem normalizál; a későbbi kód hamisan kész admitted formot kap.  
FIX SKETCH: A kódot javítanám: mindkét hook operandusfeloldása járja be minden deklarált `*` szegmenst; a wildcardot ígérő komment és a load gate már ugyanazt a nyelvet képviseli.

### F8 — A nested `deepKeyStringness` pontos finding-multiplicitása nem lett eldöntve
CLASS: UNRUN  
LENS: C  
WHAT: Nem futott le annak eldöntése, hogy egy non-string kulcs alatti újabb non-string kulcsot a dokumentált DEEP scan külön findingként köteles-e jelenteni, miközben az engine az elsőnél nem ereszkedik tovább.  
WHERE: vocabulary.ts:204-209 versus engine.ts:780-804  
EVIDENCE:
```text
unrun — egy két szintű, non-string YAML Map-kulcsokat tartalmazó file-channel próba és az elvárt finding-multiplicitás tisztázása döntené el.
```
WHY IT MATTERS: A deklarációszerző nem tudja, hogy a „DEEP scan” minden hibás kulcsot vagy csak az első elérhetetlen ágat jelenti-e.

- HEAD: `e66f58bdc76360bb86a0d9dbaf4ab4c9c9d24844`
- Darabszám: IN-SCOPE 7 / CARRIED-SCOPE 0 / UNRUN 1
- A Lens A felsorolásából nagyjából 32/34 konstrukciót vagy variánst futtattam; nem jutott külön futtatott próbához a nested `deepKeyStringness`, illetve a `nonempty.gating: true` suppression-ág.
- Nem: a deklaráció szerzője jelenleg nem bízhat abban, hogy a vocabulary doc commentjei pontosan leírják az engine viselkedését.
